import os
import sys
from datetime import datetime
from db_manager import db_instance

class PermissionManager:
    def __init__(self, db=db_instance):
        self.db = db

    def get_user(self, user_id):
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM users WHERE user_id = %s", (user_id,))
            row = cursor.fetchone()
            return dict(row) if row else None
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def upsert_user(self, user_id, staff_name, gender, is_global_super_admin=False, is_active=True, is_hr_member=True):
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            now_iso = datetime.now().isoformat()
            cursor.execute("""
            INSERT INTO users (user_id, staff_name, gender, is_global_super_admin, is_active, is_hr_member, created_at)
            VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
            ON CONFLICT(user_id) DO UPDATE SET
                staff_name = EXCLUDED.staff_name,
                gender = EXCLUDED.gender,
                is_global_super_admin = (users.is_global_super_admin OR EXCLUDED.is_global_super_admin),
                is_active = EXCLUDED.is_active,
                is_hr_member = CASE WHEN EXCLUDED.is_hr_member = FALSE THEN FALSE ELSE users.is_hr_member END;
            """, (user_id, staff_name, gender, bool(is_global_super_admin), bool(is_active), bool(is_hr_member), now_iso))
            conn.commit()
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            raise
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def update_staff_name(self, user_id, new_name):
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("UPDATE users SET staff_name = %s WHERE user_id = %s", (new_name, user_id))
            conn.commit()
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            raise
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def delete_user(self, user_id):
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("DELETE FROM users WHERE user_id = %s", (user_id,))
            conn.commit()
            return True
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            return False
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def set_user_active(self, user_id, is_active):
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("UPDATE users SET is_active = %s WHERE user_id = %s", (bool(is_active), user_id))
            conn.commit()
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            raise
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def set_global_super_admin(self, user_id, is_global):
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("UPDATE users SET is_global_super_admin = %s WHERE user_id = %s", (bool(is_global), user_id))
            conn.commit()
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            raise
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def list_all_users(self, hr_only=True):
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cond = "WHERE (u.is_hr_member = TRUE OR u.is_global_super_admin = TRUE)" if hr_only else ""
            cursor.execute(f"""
            SELECT u.*, 
                   (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM project_users pu WHERE pu.user_id = u.user_id AND pu.is_active = TRUE) as active_projects_count
            FROM users u
            {cond}
            ORDER BY u.is_global_super_admin DESC, u.user_id ASC;
            """)
            rows = cursor.fetchall()
            return [dict(r) for r in rows]
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def get_user_project_role(self, project_id, user_id):
        user = self.get_user(user_id)
        if not user or not user.get('is_active', True):
            return None, None, None, False, None
        if user.get('is_global_super_admin', False):
            return 'super_admin', user.get('gender', 'ط®ط§ظ†ظ…'), user.get('staff_name', f"ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط± {user_id}"), True, None
        if not project_id:
            return None, user.get('gender'), user.get('staff_name'), False, None

        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("""
            SELECT role, gender, is_active, assigned_unit FROM project_users 
            WHERE project_id = %s AND user_id = %s
            """, (project_id, user_id))
            row = cursor.fetchone()
        finally:
            conn.close()

        if row and row['is_active']:
            gender = row['gender'] or user.get('gender', 'ط®ط§ظ†ظ…')
            return row['role'], gender, user.get('staff_name', f"ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط± {user_id}"), True, row['assigned_unit']
        return None, user.get('gender'), user.get('staff_name'), False, None

    def list_accessible_projects(self, user_id, include_archived=False):
        user = self.get_user(user_id)
        if not user or not user.get('is_active', True):
            return []
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            if user.get('is_global_super_admin', False):
                if include_archived:
                    cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM projects ORDER BY id DESC")
                else:
                    cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM projects WHERE status = 'ACTIVE' ORDER BY id DESC")
            else:
                status_clause = "" if include_archived else "AND p.status = 'ACTIVE'"
                cursor.execute(f"""
                SELECT p.* FROM projects p
                JOIN project_users pu ON p.id = pu.project_id
                WHERE pu.user_id = %s AND pu.is_active = TRUE {status_clause}
                ORDER BY p.id DESC;
                """, (user_id,))
            rows = cursor.fetchall()
            return [dict(r) for r in rows]
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def set_project_user(self, project_id, user_id, role='user', gender=None, is_active=1, assigned_unit=None):
        user = self.get_user(user_id)
        if not user:
            self.upsert_user(user_id, f"ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط± {user_id}", gender or 'ط®ط§ظ†ظ…')
            user = self.get_user(user_id)
        user_gender = gender or user.get('gender', 'ط®ط§ظ†ظ…')
        now_iso = datetime.now().isoformat()

        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            if role == 'unit_head' and user and not user.get('is_global_super_admin'):
                cursor.execute("UPDATE users SET is_hr_member = FALSE WHERE user_id = %s AND is_global_super_admin = FALSE", (user_id,))

            cursor.execute("""
            INSERT INTO project_users (project_id, user_id, role, gender, is_active, assigned_unit, created_at)
            VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
            ON CONFLICT(project_id, user_id) DO UPDATE SET
                role = EXCLUDED.role,
                gender = EXCLUDED.gender,
                is_active = EXCLUDED.is_active,
                assigned_unit = CASE WHEN EXCLUDED.assigned_unit IS NOT NULL THEN EXCLUDED.assigned_unit ELSE project_users.assigned_unit END;
            """, (project_id, user_id, role, user_gender, bool(is_active), assigned_unit, now_iso))
            conn.commit()
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            raise
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def remove_project_user(self, project_id, user_id):
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("DELETE FROM project_users WHERE project_id = %s AND user_id = %s", (project_id, user_id))
            conn.commit()
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            raise
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def get_project_admins(self, project_id, include_global=True):
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            admin_ids = set()
            admins = []

            cursor.execute("""
            SELECT pu.user_id, pu.role, u.staff_name, u.gender, u.is_active
            FROM project_users pu
            JOIN users u ON pu.user_id = u.user_id
            WHERE pu.project_id = %s AND pu.role IN ('admin', 'super_admin') AND pu.is_active = TRUE AND u.is_active = TRUE
            """, (project_id,))
            for r in cursor.fetchall():
                d = dict(r)
                if d['user_id'] not in admin_ids:
                    admins.append(d)
                    admin_ids.add(d['user_id'])

            if include_global:
                cursor.execute("""
                SELECT user_id, 'super_admin' as role, staff_name, gender, is_active
                FROM users
                WHERE is_global_super_admin = TRUE AND is_active = TRUE
                """)
                for r in cursor.fetchall():
                    d = dict(r)
                    if d['user_id'] not in admin_ids:
                        admins.append(d)
                        admin_ids.add(d['user_id'])

            return admins
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def get_project_members(self, project_id):
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("""
            SELECT pu.user_id, pu.role, pu.is_active, pu.gender as project_gender, pu.assigned_unit,
                   u.staff_name, u.gender as user_gender, u.is_global_super_admin
            FROM project_users pu
            JOIN users u ON pu.user_id = u.user_id
            WHERE pu.project_id = %s
            ORDER BY pu.role DESC, u.staff_name ASC
            """, (project_id,))
            rows = cursor.fetchall()
            return [dict(r) for r in rows]
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def apply_gender_filter(self, items, user_id, project_id):
        role, gender, _, is_active, _ = self.get_user_project_role(project_id, user_id)
        if role in ('super_admin', 'admin'):
            return items
        return [item for item in items if str(item.get('ط¬ظ†ط³غŒطھ', item.get('gender', ''))).strip() == gender]

    def get_user_context(self, user_id):
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("SELECT current_project_id, current_session_id FROM user_context WHERE user_id = %s", (user_id,))
            row = cursor.fetchone()
            if row:
                return row['current_project_id'], row['current_session_id']
            return None, None
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def set_user_context(self, user_id, project_id=None, session_id=None):
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            now_iso = datetime.now().isoformat()
            cursor.execute("""
            INSERT INTO user_context (user_id, current_project_id, current_session_id, updated_at)
            VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s)
            ON CONFLICT(user_id) DO UPDATE SET
                current_project_id = EXCLUDED.current_project_id,
                current_session_id = EXCLUDED.current_session_id,
                updated_at = EXCLUDED.updated_at;
            """, (user_id, project_id, session_id, now_iso))
            conn.commit()
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            raise
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def list_user_assigned_projects(self, user_id):
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("""
            SELECT p.id as project_id, p.name as project_name, p.status as project_status,
                   pu.role, pu.assigned_unit, pu.is_active
            FROM project_users pu
            JOIN projects p ON pu.project_id = p.id
            WHERE pu.user_id = %s AND pu.is_active = TRUE
            ORDER BY p.id DESC
            """, (user_id,))
            rows = cursor.fetchall()
            return [dict(r) for r in rows]
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def get_available_hr_members_for_project(self, project_id):
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("""
            SELECT u.user_id, u.staff_name, u.gender, u.is_global_super_admin
            FROM users u
            WHERE (u.is_hr_member = TRUE OR u.is_global_super_admin = TRUE)
              AND u.is_active = TRUE 
              AND u.user_id NOT IN (
                  SELECT pu.user_id FROM project_users pu WHERE pu.project_id = %s AND pu.is_active = TRUE
              )
            ORDER BY u.staff_name ASC
            """, (project_id,))
            rows = cursor.fetchall()
            return [dict(r) for r in rows]
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def set_project_unit_head(self, project_id, user_id, assigned_unit, staff_name=None, gender='ط®ط§ظ†ظ…'):
        user = self.get_user(user_id)
        if not user:
            self.upsert_user(user_id, staff_name or f"ظ…ط³ط¦ظˆظ„ ظˆط§ط­ط¯ {assigned_unit}", gender, is_hr_member=False)
        else:
            if not user.get('is_global_super_admin'):
                conn = self.db.get_connection()
                try:
                    c = conn.cursor()
                    c.execute("UPDATE users SET is_hr_member = FALSE WHERE user_id = %s AND is_global_super_admin = FALSE", (user_id,))
                    conn.commit()
                except Exception:
                    conn.rollback()
                    raise
                finally:
                    conn.close()
        self.set_project_user(project_id, user_id, role='unit_head', gender=gender, assigned_unit=assigned_unit, is_active=1)

    def authorize_attendance_action(self, actor_user_id, project_id, session_id=None, staff_id=None, action="view"):
        user = self.get_user(actor_user_id)
        if not user or not user.get('is_active', True):
            return False, "ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط± ط¯ط± ط³ط§ظ…ط§ظ†ظ‡ ظپط¹ط§ظ„ ظ†غŒط³طھ", None

        if user.get('is_global_super_admin', False):
            if session_id is not None and staff_id is not None and action in ("update_attendance", "update_card", "add_late", "edit_desc"):
                conn = self.db.get_connection()
                try:
                    cursor = conn.cursor()
                    cursor.execute("SELECT 1 FROM attendance WHERE project_id = %s AND session_id = %s AND staff_id = %s", (project_id, session_id, staff_id))
                    in_roster = cursor.fetchone()
                finally:
                    conn.close()
                if not in_roster:
                    return False, "ظ†غŒط±ظˆ ط¯ط± ظ„غŒط³طھ ط­ط¶ظˆط± ظˆ ط؛غŒط§ط¨ ط§غŒظ† ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ ط¹ط¶ظˆ ظ†غŒط³طھ", "super_admin"
            return True, "ط¯ط³طھط±ط³غŒ ظ…ط¯غŒط± ط§ط±ط´ط¯ ع©ظ„ ط³ط§ظ…ط§ظ†ظ‡", "super_admin"

        if not project_id:
            return False, "ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ ظ…ط´ط®طµ ظ†ط´ط¯ظ‡ ط§ط³طھ", None

        role, actor_gender, staff_name, is_active, assigned_unit = self.get_user_project_role(project_id, actor_user_id)
        if not is_active or not role:
            return False, "ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط± ط¹ط¶ظˆ ط§غŒظ† ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ ظ†غŒط³طھ غŒط§ ط¯ط³طھط±ط³غŒ ط§ظˆ ظ…ط¹ظ„ظ‚ ط´ط¯ظ‡ ط§ط³طھ", None

        if role == 'unit_head':
            allowed_unit_actions = ["view_unit_staff", "view_unit_attendance", "request_shortage", "view"]
            if action not in allowed_unit_actions and not action.startswith("view"):
                return False, "ظ…ط³ط¦ظˆظ„ ظˆط§ط­ط¯ ظ…ط¬ط§ط² ط¨ظ‡ طھط؛غŒغŒط± ظ…ط³طھظ‚غŒظ… ط­ط¶ظˆط± غŒط§ ع©ط§ط±طھ ظ†غŒط³طھ", role

        if session_id is not None:
            conn = self.db.get_connection()
            try:
                cursor = conn.cursor()
                cursor.execute("SELECT id, project_id, status FROM sessions WHERE id = %s", (session_id,))
                sess_row = cursor.fetchone()
            finally:
                conn.close()
            if not sess_row or sess_row['project_id'] != project_id:
                return False, "ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ ظ…طھط¹ظ„ظ‚ ط¨ظ‡ ط§غŒظ† ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ ظ†غŒط³طھ", role
            if sess_row['status'] == 'CANCELLED' and action in ("update_attendance", "update_card", "add_late", "edit_desc"):
                return False, "ط§ظ…ع©ط§ظ† ط«ط¨طھ ظˆط¶ط¹غŒطھ ط¯ط± ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ ظ„ط؛ظˆ ط´ط¯ظ‡ ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ ظ†ط¯ط§ط±ط¯", role

        if staff_id is not None:
            from staff_manager import staff_manager
            staff = staff_manager.get_staff_member(staff_id)
            if not staff or staff.get('project_id') != project_id:
                return False, "ظ†غŒط±ظˆ ظ…طھط¹ظ„ظ‚ ط¨ظ‡ ط§غŒظ† ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ ظ†غŒط³طھ", role

            staff_gender = str(staff.get('gender', '')).strip()
            staff_unit = str(staff.get('unit', '')).strip()

            if role == 'unit_head':
                if assigned_unit and staff_unit != assigned_unit:
                    return False, f"ط¯ط³طھط±ط³غŒ ط´ظ…ط§ ظپظ‚ط· ظ…ط­ط¯ظˆط¯ ط¨ظ‡ ظˆط§ط­ط¯ آ«{assigned_unit}آ» ط§ط³طھ", role

            if role in ('operator', 'user'):
                if actor_gender and staff_gender and actor_gender != staff_gender:
                    return False, f"ط¹ط¯ظ… طھط·ط§ط¨ظ‚ ط¬ظ†ط³غŒطھغŒ: ط§ظ¾ط±ط§طھظˆط± {actor_gender} ظ…ط¬ط§ط² ط¨ظ‡ طھط؛غŒغŒط± ظˆط¶ط¹غŒطھ ظ†غŒط±ظˆغŒ {staff_gender} ظ†غŒط³طھ", role

            if session_id is not None and action in ("update_attendance", "update_card", "add_late", "edit_desc", "view_staff_attendance"):
                conn = self.db.get_connection()
                try:
                    cursor = conn.cursor()
                    cursor.execute("""
                    SELECT 1 FROM attendance 
                    WHERE project_id = %s AND session_id = %s AND staff_id = %s
                    """, (project_id, session_id, staff_id))
                    in_roster = cursor.fetchone()

                    cursor.execute("SELECT schedule_id FROM sessions WHERE id = %s", (session_id,))
                    s_row = cursor.fetchone()
                    sched_id = s_row['schedule_id'] if s_row else None
                    sched_ok = True
                    if sched_id is not None:
                        cursor.execute("""
                        SELECT 1 FROM schedule_staff 
                        WHERE schedule_id = %s AND staff_id = %s AND is_active = TRUE
                        """, (sched_id, staff_id))
                        sched_ok = bool(cursor.fetchone())
                finally:
                    conn.close()

                if not in_roster or not sched_ok:
                    return False, "ظ†غŒط±ظˆ ط¯ط± ظ„غŒط³طھ ع©ط§ط¯ط± ظ…ط¬ط§ط² ط§غŒظ† ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡/ع©ظ„ط§ط³ ط¹ط¶ظˆ ظ†غŒط³طھ", role

        return True, "ط¯ط³طھط±ط³غŒ ظ…ط¬ط§ط²", role

    def record_operator_daily_absence(self, project_id, user_id, absent_date=None):
        dt = absent_date or datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        now_iso = datetime.now().isoformat()
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("""
            INSERT INTO operator_daily_absence (project_id, user_id, absent_date, created_at)
            VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s)
            ON CONFLICT(project_id, user_id, absent_date) DO NOTHING;
            """, (project_id, user_id, dt, now_iso))
            conn.commit()
            return True
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            raise
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def clear_operator_daily_absence(self, project_id, user_id, absent_date=None):
        dt = absent_date or datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("""
            DELETE FROM operator_daily_absence
            WHERE project_id = %s AND user_id = %s AND absent_date = %s;
            """, (project_id, user_id, dt))
            conn.commit()
            return True
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            raise
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def is_operator_absent_today(self, project_id, user_id, date_str=None):
        dt = date_str or datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("""
            SELECT 1 FROM operator_daily_absence
            WHERE project_id = %s AND user_id = %s AND absent_date = %s;
            """, (project_id, user_id, dt))
            row = cursor.fetchone()
            return bool(row)
        finally:
            conn.close()

permission_manager = PermissionManager()
=== project_manager.py ===
import os
import sys
import logging
import shutil
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from config import PROJECT_FILES_DIR, ARCHIVES_DIR, BASE_PATH
from db_manager import db_instance

class ProjectManager:
    def __init__(self, db=db_instance):
        self.db = db

    def create_project(self, name, project_type='ط¹ظ…ظˆظ…غŒ', description='', excel_path=None,
                       total_sessions=0, recurring_days='', activation_time='',
                       start_date='', end_date='', has_prep_day=0):
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        now_iso = datetime.now().isoformat()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute('''
            INSERT INTO projects (
                name, type, description, status, excel_path, 
                total_sessions, recurring_days, activation_time, 
                start_date, end_date, has_prep_day, 
                created_at, updated_at
            )
            VALUES (%s, %s, %s, 'ACTIVE', %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
            RETURNING id;
            ''', (
                name.strip(), project_type.strip(), description.strip(), excel_path,
                int(total_sessions or 0), str(recurring_days or '').strip(), str(activation_time or '').strip(),
                str(start_date or '').strip(), str(end_date or '').strip(), bool(has_prep_day),
                now_iso, now_iso
            ))
            project_id = cursor.fetchone()['id']
            conn.commit()
        except Exception as e:
            conn.rollback()
            raise e
        finally:
            conn.close()

        proj_dir = os.path.join(PROJECT_FILES_DIR, f"project_{project_id}")
        os.makedirs(proj_dir, exist_ok=True)

        template_file = os.path.join(BASE_PATH, "ظ‚ط§ظ„ط¨ ط§ع©ط³ظ„ ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡.xlsx")
        target_file = os.path.join(proj_dir, "project_data.xlsx")

        if not excel_path and os.path.exists(template_file):
            shutil.copy2(template_file, target_file)
            try:
                import openpyxl
                wb = openpyxl.load_workbook(target_file)
                for sname in ['ط±ظˆط² ط¢ظ…ط§ط¯ظ‡ ط³ط§ط²غŒ', 'ط±ظˆط² 1', 'ط±ظˆط² 2']:
                    if sname in wb.sheetnames and len(wb.sheetnames) > 1:
                        del wb[sname]
                wb.save(target_file)
                wb.close()
            except Exception as ex_err:
                logging.error(f"Failed to prepare project template Excel: {ex_err}")
                self.delete_project(project_id)
                raise RuntimeError(f"ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط¢ظ…ط§ط¯ظ‡â€Œط³ط§ط²غŒ ظ‚ط§ظ„ط¨ ط§ع©ط³ظ„ ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡: {ex_err}")

            self.update_project_excel_path(project_id, target_file)
            try:
                from excel_manager import excel_manager
                success = excel_manager.import_project_excel(project_id, target_file)
                if not success:
                    raise RuntimeError("ط§ط¹طھط¨ط§ط±ط³ظ†ط¬غŒ ظ‚ط§ظ„ط¨ ط§ع©ط³ظ„ ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ ط¨ط§ ط´ع©ط³طھ ظ…ظˆط§ط¬ظ‡ ط´ط¯.")
            except Exception as imp_err:
                logging.error(f"Initial project Excel import failed: {imp_err}")
                self.delete_project(project_id)
                raise RuntimeError(f"ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط¨ط§ط±ع¯ط°ط§ط±غŒ ط§ظˆظ„غŒظ‡ ط§ع©ط³ظ„ ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡: {imp_err}")

        return project_id

    def create_project_with_smart_sessions(self, name, project_type='ط¹ظ…ظˆظ…غŒ', description='',
                                           total_sessions=0, recurring_days='', activation_time='',
                                           start_date='', end_date='', has_prep_day=0):
        from attendance_manager import attendance_manager

        project_id = self.create_project(
            name=name, project_type=project_type, description=description,
            total_sessions=total_sessions, recurring_days=recurring_days, activation_time=activation_time,
            start_date=start_date, end_date=end_date, has_prep_day=has_prep_day
        )

        first_session_id = None
        num_sessions = int(total_sessions) if total_sessions and int(total_sessions) > 0 else 1

        base_dt = None
        if start_date:
            try:
                base_dt = datetime.strptime(start_date.strip(), "%Y-%m-%d")
            except ValueError:
                base_dt = datetime.now()
        else:
            base_dt = datetime.now()

        if project_type == 'ع©ظ„ط§ط³':
            schedule_name = f"ع©ظ„ط§ط³ {name}"
            sched_id = attendance_manager.create_schedule(
                project_id, name=schedule_name, day_of_week=recurring_days or "ظ‡ظپطھع¯غŒ",
                time_str=activation_time or "16:00"
            )
            
            class_start_dt = base_dt
            valid_days = ["ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "غŒع©ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "ط³ظ‡â€Œط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "ع†ظ‡ط§ط±ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "ظ¾ظ†ط¬â€Œط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "ط¬ظ…ط¹ظ‡"]
            if recurring_days and recurring_days.strip() in valid_days:
                from utils import get_persian_weekday
                for offset in range(7):
                    candidate = base_dt + timedelta(days=offset)
                    if get_persian_weekday(candidate) == recurring_days.strip():
                        class_start_dt = candidate
                        break

            for i in range(1, num_sessions + 1):
                sess_dt = class_start_dt + timedelta(days=7 * (i - 1))
                sess_date_str = sess_dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
                sess_id = attendance_manager.create_session(
                    project_id, name=f"ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ {i}", session_date=sess_date_str,
                    time_str=activation_time, day_of_week=recurring_days,
                    schedule_id=sched_id, copy_from_prev_session=True, sync_excel_sheet=True
                )
                if i == 1:
                    first_session_id = sess_id

        else:
            if has_prep_day:
                prep_dt = base_dt - timedelta(days=1)
                prep_id = attendance_manager.create_session(
                    project_id, name="ط±ظˆط² ط¢ظ…ط§ط¯ظ‡ ط³ط§ط²غŒ", session_date=prep_dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%d"),
                    copy_from_prev_session=True, sync_excel_sheet=True
                )
                first_session_id = prep_id

            for i in range(1, num_sessions + 1):
                day_dt = base_dt + timedelta(days=(i - 1))
                sess_id = attendance_manager.create_session(
                    project_id, name=f"ط±ظˆط² {i}", session_date=day_dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%d"),
                    copy_from_prev_session=True, sync_excel_sheet=True
                )
                if first_session_id is None:
                    first_session_id = sess_id

        try:
            from excel_manager import excel_manager
            conn = self.db.get_connection()
            try:
                cursor = conn.cursor()
                cursor.execute("SELECT name FROM sessions WHERE project_id = %s", (project_id,))
                active_s_names = [r['name'] for r in cursor.fetchall()]
            finally:
                conn.close()
            excel_manager.cleanup_sample_sheets(project_id, active_s_names)
        except Exception:
            pass

        return project_id, first_session_id

    def get_project(self, project_id):
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM projects WHERE id = %s", (project_id,))
            row = cursor.fetchone()
            return dict(row) if row else None
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def get_project_by_name(self, name):
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM projects WHERE name = %s", (name.strip(),))
            row = cursor.fetchone()
            return dict(row) if row else None
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def list_projects(self, status=None):
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            if status:
                cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM projects WHERE status = %s ORDER BY id DESC", (status,))
            else:
                cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM projects ORDER BY id DESC")
            rows = cursor.fetchall()
            return [dict(r) for r in rows]
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def update_project_status(self, project_id, new_status):
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            now_iso = datetime.now().isoformat()
            cursor.execute('''
            UPDATE projects SET status = %s, updated_at = %s WHERE id = %s
            ''', (new_status, now_iso, project_id))
            conn.commit()
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            raise
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def update_project_excel_path(self, project_id, excel_path):
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            now_iso = datetime.now().isoformat()
            cursor.execute('''
            UPDATE projects SET excel_path = %s, updated_at = %s WHERE id = %s
            ''', (excel_path, now_iso, project_id))
            conn.commit()
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            raise
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def archive_project(self, project_id):
        self.update_project_status(project_id, 'ARCHIVED')

    def unarchive_project(self, project_id):
        self.update_project_status(project_id, 'ACTIVE')

    def update_project_details(self, project_id, name=None, description=None, project_type=None):
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            now_iso = datetime.now().isoformat()
            updates = []
            params = []
            if name is not None:
                updates.append("name = %s")
                params.append(str(name).strip())
            if description is not None:
                updates.append("description = %s")
                params.append(str(description).strip())
            if project_type is not None:
                updates.append("type = %s")
                params.append(str(project_type).strip())
            if updates:
                updates.append("updated_at = %s")
                params.append(now_iso)
                params.append(project_id)
                sql = f"UPDATE projects SET {', '.join(updates)} WHERE id = %s"
                cursor.execute(sql, tuple(params))
                conn.commit()
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            raise
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def delete_project(self, project_id):
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("DELETE FROM attendance WHERE staff_id IN (SELECT id FROM project_staff WHERE project_id = %s)", (project_id,))
            cursor.execute("DELETE FROM project_staff WHERE project_id = %s", (project_id,))
            cursor.execute("DELETE FROM sessions WHERE project_id = %s", (project_id,))
            cursor.execute("DELETE FROM schedules WHERE project_id = %s", (project_id,))
            cursor.execute("DELETE FROM org_chart WHERE project_id = %s", (project_id,))
            cursor.execute("DELETE FROM shortages WHERE project_id = %s", (project_id,))
            cursor.execute("DELETE FROM project_users WHERE project_id = %s", (project_id,))
            cursor.execute("DELETE FROM staff_logs WHERE project_id = %s", (project_id,))
            cursor.execute("UPDATE user_context SET current_project_id = NULL, current_session_id = NULL WHERE current_project_id = %s", (project_id,))
            cursor.execute("DELETE FROM projects WHERE id = %s", (project_id,))
            conn.commit()
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            raise
        finally:
            conn.close()

        proj_dir = os.path.join(PROJECT_FILES_DIR, f"project_{project_id}")
        if os.path.exists(proj_dir):
            try:
                shutil.rmtree(proj_dir)
            except Exception:
                pass
        return True

    def set_project_org_chart(self, project_id, unit_section_pairs, cursor=None):
        own_conn = None
        if cursor is None:
            own_conn = self.db.get_connection()
            cursor = own_conn.cursor()
        try:
            cursor.execute("DELETE FROM org_chart WHERE project_id = %s", (project_id,))
            for idx, item in enumerate(unit_section_pairs, start=1):
                if len(item) == 3:
                    unit, section, order_val = item
                else:
                    unit, section = item
                    order_val = idx
                u = str(unit or '').strip()
                s = str(section or '').strip()
                if u and u not in ('None', ''):
                    cursor.execute("INSERT INTO org_chart (project_id, unit, section, display_order) VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s)", (project_id, u, s, int(order_val)))
            if own_conn:
                own_conn.commit()
        except Exception:
            if own_conn:
                own_conn.rollback()
            raise
        finally:
            if own_conn:
                own_conn.close()

    def get_project_org_chart(self, project_id):
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("SELECT unit, section, display_order FROM org_chart WHERE project_id = %s ORDER BY display_order ASC, id ASC", (project_id,))
            rows = cursor.fetchall()
        finally:
            conn.close()
        chart = {}
        for r in rows:
            u = r['unit']
            s = r['section']
            if u not in chart:
                chart[u] = []
            if s and s not in ('None', '') and s not in chart[u]:
                chart[u].append(s)
        return chart

import os
import sys
from datetime import datetime
from db_manager import db_instance
from attendance_manager import attendance_manager
from utils import safe_markdown, normalize_persian


class ClassReportDataBuilder:
    """Extracts, validates, and organizes data for class text reports."""
    @staticmethod
    def build(project_id, session_id):
        from project_manager import project_manager

        proj = project_manager.get_project(project_id)
        sess = attendance_manager.get_session(session_id)
        all_att_raw = attendance_manager.get_session_attendance(project_id, session_id)
        org_chart = project_manager.get_project_org_chart(project_id)

        # ظپغŒظ„طھط± ط·ظ„ط§غŒغŒ: ط­ط°ظپ ظ†غŒط±ظˆظ‡ط§غŒ آ«ط¨ط¯ظˆظ† ط´غŒظپطھآ» ظپظ‚ط· ط§ط² ع¯ط²ط§ط±ط´ ظ…طھظ†غŒ
        all_att = [s for s in all_att_raw if "ط¨ط¯ظˆظ† ط´غŒظپطھ" not in str(s.get('status', '')).strip()]

        proj_name = proj['name'] if proj else "ع©ظ„ط§ط³"
        sess_date = sess.get('session_date') or datetime.now().strftime("%Y/%m/%d") if sess else ""
        sess_day = sess.get('day_of_week') or "" if sess else ""

        # 1. Identify Senior / Arshad staff
        arshad_staff = None
        for s in all_att:
            pos = str(s.get('position', '')).strip()
            sec = str(s.get('section', '')).strip()
            u = str(s.get('unit', '')).strip()
            if "ط§ط±ط´ط¯" in pos or "ط§ط±ط´ط¯" in sec or "ط§ط±ط´ط¯" in u:
                arshad_staff = s
                break

        # 2. Group staff by unit, section, and hierarchy
        units_data = {}
        for s in all_att:
            if s == arshad_staff:
                continue

            u_raw = str(s.get('unit') or '').strip()
            if not u_raw or u_raw == 'None':
                u_raw = 'ط¹ظ…ظˆظ…غŒ'

            sec_raw = str(s.get('section') or '').strip()

            if sec_raw in ['None', '', 'ط¹ظ…ظˆظ…غŒ', 'ط§طµظ„غŒ', '-'] or sec_raw == u_raw:
                sec_raw = ''

            pos_raw = str(s.get('position') or '').strip()

            if u_raw not in units_data:
                units_data[u_raw] = {'heads': [], 'deputies': [], 'sections': {}, 'general_staff': []}

            if pos_raw in ["ظ…ط³ط¦ظˆظ„ ظˆط§ط­ط¯", "ظ…ط¯غŒط±", "ظ…ط¯غŒط± ظˆط§ط­ط¯"]:
                units_data[u_raw]['heads'].append(s)
            elif pos_raw in ["ط¬ط§ظ†ط´غŒظ†", "ظ…ط¹ط§ظˆظ†", "ط¬ط§ظ†ط´غŒظ† ظ…ط³ط¦ظˆظ„ ظˆط§ط­ط¯", "ط¬ط§ظ†ط´غŒظ† ظˆط§ط­ط¯", "ط¬ط§ظ†ط´غŒظ† ظ…ط³ط¦ظˆظ„"]:
                units_data[u_raw]['deputies'].append(s)
            elif pos_raw in ["ظ…ط³ط¦ظˆظ„ ط¨ط®ط´", "ط³ط±ظ¾ط±ط³طھ ط¨ط®ط´", "ط³ط±ظ¾ط±ط³طھ"]:
                if sec_raw:
                    if sec_raw not in units_data[u_raw]['sections']:
                        units_data[u_raw]['sections'][sec_raw] = {'heads': [], 'staff': []}
                    units_data[u_raw]['sections'][sec_raw]['heads'].append(s)
                else:
                    units_data[u_raw]['general_staff'].append(s)
            elif pos_raw == "ظ…ط³ط¦ظˆظ„":
                if not sec_raw:
                    units_data[u_raw]['heads'].append(s)
                else:
                    if sec_raw not in units_data[u_raw]['sections']:
                        units_data[u_raw]['sections'][sec_raw] = {'heads': [], 'staff': []}
                    units_data[u_raw]['sections'][sec_raw]['heads'].append(s)
            else:
                if sec_raw:
                    if sec_raw not in units_data[u_raw]['sections']:
                        units_data[u_raw]['sections'][sec_raw] = {'heads': [], 'staff': []}
                    units_data[u_raw]['sections'][sec_raw]['staff'].append(s)
                else:
                    units_data[u_raw]['general_staff'].append(s)

        ordered_units = list(org_chart.keys())
        for u in units_data.keys():
            if u not in ordered_units:
                ordered_units.append(u)

        return {
            'project_name': proj_name,
            'session_date': sess_date,
            'session_day': sess_day,
            'arshad': arshad_staff,
            'ordered_units': ordered_units,
            'org_chart': org_chart,
            'units_data': units_data
        }


class PersianClassReportRenderer:
    """Renders validated class report data into the required Persian business format with emojis and Shamsi date."""

    @staticmethod
    def to_persian_digits(text):
        persian_digits = "غ°غ±غ²غ³غ´غµغ¶غ·غ¸غ¹"
        english_digits = "0123456789"
        translation_table = str.maketrans(english_digits, persian_digits)
        return str(text).translate(translation_table)

    @staticmethod
    def gregorian_to_jalali(gy, gm, gd):
        g_d_m = [0, 31, 59, 90, 120, 151, 181, 212, 243, 273, 304, 334]
        gy -= 1600
        gm -= 1
        gd -= 1

        g_day_no = 365 * gy + (gy + 3) // 4 - (gy + 99) // 100 + (gy + 399) // 400

        for i in range(gm):
            g_day_no += g_d_m[i + 1] - g_d_m[i]

        if gm > 1 and ((gy % 4 == 0 and gy % 100 != 0) or (gy % 400 == 0)):
            g_day_no += 1

        g_day_no += gd
        j_day_no = g_day_no - 79

        j_np = j_day_no // 12053
        j_day_no %= 12053

        jy = 979 + 33 * j_np + 4 * (j_day_no // 1461)
        j_day_no %= 1461

        if j_day_no >= 366:
            jy += (j_day_no - 1) // 365
            j_day_no = (j_day_no - 1) % 365

        for i in range(11):
            days_in_month = 31 if i < 6 else 30
            if j_day_no < days_in_month:
                jm = i + 1
                jd = j_day_no + 1
                break
            j_day_no -= days_in_month
        else:
            jm = 12
            jd = j_day_no + 1

        return jy, jm, jd

    @staticmethod
    def convert_date(date_str):
        try:
            parts = date_str.replace('/', '-').split('-')
            if len(parts) == 3 and int(parts[0]) > 1900:
                jy, jm, jd = PersianClassReportRenderer.gregorian_to_jalali(int(parts[0]), int(parts[1]), int(parts[2]))
                return f"{jy}/{jm:02d}/{jd:02d}"
        except Exception:
            pass
        return date_str

    @staticmethod
    def render(report_data):
        proj_name = report_data['project_name']
        raw_date = PersianClassReportRenderer.convert_date(report_data['session_date'])
        sess_date = PersianClassReportRenderer.to_persian_digits(raw_date)
        sess_day = report_data.get('session_day', '')
        arshad = report_data['arshad']
        ordered_units = report_data['ordered_units']
        org_chart = report_data['org_chart']
        units_data = report_data['units_data']

        def format_staff(s):
            name = s.get('name', '')
            st = str(s.get('status', '')).strip()

            status_icon = "âڑھï¸ڈ"
            if "ط­ط§ط¶ط±" in st:
                status_icon = "âœ…"
            elif "ط؛ط§غŒط¨" in st:
                status_icon = "â‌Œ"
            elif "طھط§ط®غŒط±" in st:
                status_icon = "âڈ±"

            return f"{status_icon} {name}"

        lines = [f"ع©ظ„ط§ط³ ط³ط·ط­: {proj_name}       ط±ظˆط²: {sess_day}         طھط§ط±غŒط®: {sess_date}\n"]

        if arshad:
            lines.append(f"*ط§ط±ط´ط¯:* {format_staff(arshad)}")
            if arshad.get('phone'):
                phone_fa = PersianClassReportRenderer.to_persian_digits(arshad.get('phone', ''))
                lines.append(phone_fa)
        else:
            lines.append("*ط§ط±ط´ط¯:* ")
            lines.append("غ°غ¹...")
        lines.append("")

        dividers = ["ًں”¹ًں”¹ًں”¹ًں”¹ًں”¹ًں”¹ًں”¹ًں”¹ًں”¹ًں”¹", "ًں”¸ًں”¸ًں”¸ًں”¸ًں”¸ًں”¸ًں”¸ًں”¸ًں”¸ًں”¸"]
        div_idx = 0

        for u_name in ordered_units:
            u_data = units_data.get(u_name)
            if not u_data and u_name in org_chart:
                u_data = {'heads': [], 'deputies': [], 'sections': {}, 'general_staff': []}

            if not u_data:
                continue

            has_staff = bool(u_data.get('heads') or u_data.get('deputies') or u_data.get('general_staff'))
            if not has_staff:
                for sec_data in u_data.get('sections', {}).values():
                    if sec_data.get('heads') or sec_data.get('staff'):
                        has_staff = True
                        break

            if not has_staff:
                continue

            lines.append(dividers[div_idx])
            div_idx = 1 - div_idx
            lines.append("")

            display_unit_name = u_name if "ظˆط§ط­ط¯" in u_name else f"ظˆط§ط­ط¯ {u_name}"
            head_str = ""
            if u_data['heads']:
                head_str = " ".join([format_staff(h) for h in u_data['heads']])

            lines.append(f"*{display_unit_name}:* {head_str}".strip())

            deputy_str = ""
            if u_data['deputies']:
                deputy_str = " ".join([format_staff(d) for d in u_data['deputies']])

            if deputy_str or u_data['deputies']:
                lines.append(f"ط¬ط§ظ†ط´غŒظ† ظ…ط³ط¦ظˆظ„ ظˆط§ط­ط¯: {deputy_str}".strip())
            else:
                lines.append("ط¬ط§ظ†ط´غŒظ† ظ…ط³ط¦ظˆظ„ ظˆط§ط­ط¯:")
            lines.append("")

            sections_in_chart = org_chart.get(u_name, [])
            all_unit_sections = list(sections_in_chart)
            for sec in u_data['sections'].keys():
                if sec not in all_unit_sections:
                    all_unit_sections.append(sec)

            valid_secs = [sec for sec in all_unit_sections if sec and sec not in ('-', 'None', 'ط¹ظ…ظˆظ…غŒ', 'ط§طµظ„غŒ', u_name)]

            if valid_secs:
                for sec in valid_secs:
                    sec_data = u_data['sections'].get(sec, {'heads': [], 'staff': []})

                    sec_head_str = ""
                    if sec_data['heads']:
                        sec_head_str = " ".join([format_staff(sh) for sh in sec_data['heads']])

                    if sec_head_str:
                        lines.append(f"ط¨ط®ط´ {sec}:\nظ…ط³ط¦ظˆظ„ ط¨ط®ط´: {sec_head_str}".strip())
                    else:
                        lines.append(f"ط¨ط®ط´ {sec}:".strip())

                    lines.append("ظ†غŒط±ظˆظ‡ط§:")
                    if sec_data['staff']:
                        for st in sec_data['staff']:
                            lines.append(f"  {format_staff(st)}")
                    lines.append("")

            if u_data['general_staff']:
                lines.append("ظ†غŒط±ظˆ:")
                for st in u_data['general_staff']:
                    lines.append(f"  {format_staff(st)}")
                lines.append("")

        return "\n".join(lines).strip()


class ReportManager:
    def __init__(self, db=db_instance):
        self.db = db

    def get_dashboard_stats(self, project_id, session_id):
        all_att = attendance_manager.get_session_attendance(project_id, session_id)
        stats = {}
        total_exp = 0
        total_prs = 0

        for u in all_att:
            unit = u.get("unit", "ظ†ط§ظ…ط´ط®طµ")
            if not unit or unit == "None":
                continue
            unit_safe = safe_markdown(unit)
            if unit_safe not in stats:
                stats[unit_safe] = {'exp': 0, 'prs_m': 0, 'prs_f': 0, 'abs': 0, 'cg': 0, 'cr': 0}

            st = str(u.get("status", "")).strip()
            cd = str(u.get("card_status", "")).strip()
            g = str(u.get("gender", "")).strip()

            if "ط¨ط¯ظˆظ† ط´غŒظپطھ" not in st:
                stats[unit_safe]['exp'] += 1
                total_exp += 1
                if "ط­ط§ط¶ط±" in st or "طھط§ط®غŒط±" in st:
                    if g == "ط¢ظ‚ط§":
                        stats[unit_safe]['prs_m'] += 1
                    else:
                        stats[unit_safe]['prs_f'] += 1
                    total_prs += 1
                elif "ط؛ط§غŒط¨" in st or not st:
                    stats[unit_safe]['abs'] += 1

            if "طھط­ظˆغŒظ„ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡ ط´ط¯" in cd:
                stats[unit_safe]['cg'] += 1
            elif "ظ¾ط³ ع¯ط±ظپطھظ‡ ط´ط¯" in cd or "طھط­ظˆغŒظ„ ع¯ط±ظپطھظ‡ ط´ط¯" in cd:
                stats[unit_safe]['cr'] += 1

        overall_pct = int((total_prs / total_exp * 100)) if total_exp > 0 else 0
        return {
            'unit_stats': stats,
            'total_expected': total_exp,
            'total_present': total_prs,
            'overall_percentage': overall_pct
        }

    def log_staff_action(self, project_id, user_id, action_type):
        today = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        now_iso = datetime.now()
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("""
            INSERT INTO staff_logs (project_id, user_id, action_type, log_date, created_at)
            VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
            """, (project_id, user_id, action_type, today, now_iso))
            conn.commit()
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def get_staff_performance_report(self, project_id=None, date_str=None):
        target_date = date_str or datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            query = """
            SELECT l.user_id, l.action_type, COUNT(*) as cnt, u.staff_name
            FROM staff_logs l
            LEFT JOIN users u ON l.user_id = u.user_id
            WHERE l.log_date = %s
            """
            params = [target_date]
            if project_id:
                query += " AND l.project_id = %s"
                params.append(project_id)
            query += " GROUP BY l.user_id, l.action_type, u.staff_name"
            cursor.execute(query, params)
            rows = cursor.fetchall()
            return [dict(r) for r in rows]
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def generate_class_text_report(self, project_id, session_id):
        report_data = ClassReportDataBuilder.build(project_id, session_id)
        return PersianClassReportRenderer.render(report_data)

import os
import sys
from datetime import datetime
from db_manager import db_instance


class ShortageManager:
    def __init__(self, db=db_instance):
        self.db = db

    def add_shortage(self, project_id, unit, section, count, target_group, description):
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        now_iso = datetime.now()
        created_ids = []
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            for _ in range(int(count)):
                cursor.execute('''
                INSERT INTO shortages 
                (project_id, unit, section, count, target_group, description, status, is_notified, created_at)
                VALUES (%s, %s, %s, 1, %s, %s, 'طھط§ظ…غŒظ† ظ†ط´ط¯ظ‡', 0, %s)
                RETURNING id;
                ''', (project_id, unit.strip(), section.strip(), target_group.strip(), description.strip(), now_iso))
                created_ids.append(cursor.fetchone()['id'])
            conn.commit()
            return created_ids
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            return []
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def get_unnotified_shortages(self):
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute('''
            SELECT s.*, p.name as project_name 
            FROM shortages s
            JOIN projects p ON s.project_id = p.id
            WHERE s.is_notified = 0 AND s.status = 'طھط§ظ…غŒظ† ظ†ط´ط¯ظ‡' AND p.status = 'ACTIVE'
            ''')
            rows = cursor.fetchall()
            return [dict(r) for r in rows]
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def mark_shortage_notified(self, shortage_id):
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("UPDATE shortages SET is_notified = 1 WHERE id = %s", (shortage_id,))
            conn.commit()
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            raise
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def get_all_unresolved_shortages(self, project_id=None):
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            if project_id:
                cursor.execute('''
                SELECT s.*, p.name as project_name
                FROM shortages s
                JOIN projects p ON s.project_id = p.id
                WHERE s.project_id = %s AND s.status IN ('طھط§ظ…غŒظ† ظ†ط´ط¯ظ‡', 'ط¯ط± ط§ظ†طھط¸ط§ط± طھط§غŒغŒط¯', 'ط¯ط± ط§ظ†طھط¸ط§ط± طھط§غŒغŒط¯ ط§ط¯ظ…غŒظ†')
                ORDER BY s.id DESC
                ''', (project_id,))
            else:
                cursor.execute('''
                SELECT s.*, p.name as project_name
                FROM shortages s
                JOIN projects p ON s.project_id = p.id
                WHERE s.status IN ('طھط§ظ…غŒظ† ظ†ط´ط¯ظ‡', 'ط¯ط± ط§ظ†طھط¸ط§ط± طھط§غŒغŒط¯', 'ط¯ط± ط§ظ†طھط¸ط§ط± طھط§غŒغŒط¯ ط§ط¯ظ…غŒظ†')
                ORDER BY s.id DESC
                ''')
            rows = cursor.fetchall()
            return [dict(r) for r in rows]
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def suggest_shortage(self, shortage_id, candidate_name, candidate_phone):
        conn = self.db.get_connection()

            UPDATE shortages 
            SET status = 'ط¯ط± ط§ظ†طھط¸ط§ط± طھط§غŒغŒط¯', assigned_name = %s, phone = %s

            ''', (candidate_name.strip(), str(candidate_phone).strip(), shortage_id))

        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            return False
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def reject_shortage(self, shortage_id):

            UPDATE shortages 
            SET status = 'طھط§ظ…غŒظ† ظ†ط´ط¯ظ‡', assigned_name = NULL, phone = NULL 
            WHERE id = %s
            ''', (shortage_id,))
            conn.commit()

        except Exception:

    def get_shortage(self, shortage_id):

            RETURNING id;
            ''', (project_id, unit.strip(), section.strip(), int(count), target_group.strip(), description.strip(), requested_by, now_iso))
            sh_id = cursor.fetchone()['id']
            conn.commit()
            return sh_id
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            return None
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def approve_unit_head_shortage(self, shortage_id):

            conn.commit()
            return True
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            return False
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def update_shortage_count(self, shortage_id, new_count):
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("UPDATE shortages SET count = %s WHERE id = %s", (int(new_count), shortage_id))
            conn.commit()
            return True
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            return False
        finally:
            conn.close()

import os
import sys
import logging
from datetime import datetime
from db_manager import db_instance
from utils import normalize_persian


class StaffManager:
    def __init__(self, db=db_instance):
        self.db = db

    def add_staff_member(self, project_id, name, phone="", unit="", section="", position='ظ†غŒط±ظˆ',
                         card_title=None, shift_time='', gender='', notes='',
                         is_multi_section='ط®غŒط±', excel_row=None, staff_code=None,
                         start_session_id=None, end_session_id=None, schedule_id=None):
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        c_title = card_title if card_title is not None else section
        clean_code = str(staff_code).strip() if staff_code else None

        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            if schedule_id is not None:
                cursor.execute(
                    "SELECT id FROM schedules WHERE id = %s AND project_id = %s AND is_active = TRUE",
                    (schedule_id, project_id)
                )
                if not cursor.fetchone():
                    raise ValueError(
                        f"ط¨ط±ظ†ط§ظ…ظ‡ {schedule_id} ظ…طھط¹ظ„ظ‚ ط¨ظ‡ ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ {project_id} ظ†غŒط³طھ."
                    )

            staff_id = None
            if clean_code:
                cursor.execute(
                    "SELECT id FROM project_staff WHERE project_id = %s AND staff_code = %s",
                    (project_id, clean_code)
                )
                existing = cursor.fetchone()
                if existing:
                    staff_id = existing['id']
                    cursor.execute("""
                    UPDATE project_staff SET
                        name = %s, phone = COALESCE(NULLIF(%s, ''), phone),
                        unit = %s, section = %s, position = %s,
                        card_title = %s, shift_time = COALESCE(NULLIF(%s, ''), shift_time),
                        gender = COALESCE(NULLIF(%s, ''), gender),
                        notes = COALESCE(NULLIF(%s, ''), notes),
                        is_active = TRUE
                    WHERE id = %s;
                    """, (
                        name.strip(), str(phone or '').strip(), unit.strip(), section.strip(), position.strip(),
                        str(c_title).strip(), str(shift_time or '').strip(), gender.strip(), notes.strip(), staff_id
                    ))

            if not staff_id:
                cursor.execute("""
                INSERT INTO project_staff 
                (project_id, staff_code, name, phone, unit, section, position, card_title, shift_time, gender, is_active, notes, is_multi_section, start_session_id, end_session_id, _excel_row)
                VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, TRUE, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
                RETURNING id;
                """, (
                    project_id, clean_code, name.strip(), str(phone or '').strip(), unit.strip(), section.strip(),
                    position.strip(), str(c_title).strip(), str(shift_time or '').strip(),
                    gender.strip(), notes.strip(), is_multi_section, start_session_id, end_session_id, excel_row
                ))
                staff_id = cursor.fetchone()['id']

                if not clean_code:
                    auto_code = f"STF-{staff_id:05d}"
                    cursor.execute(
                        "UPDATE project_staff SET staff_code = %s WHERE id = %s",
                        (auto_code, staff_id)
                    )

            if schedule_id is not None:
                now_iso = datetime.now()
                cursor.execute("""
                INSERT INTO schedule_staff (schedule_id, staff_id, start_session_id, end_session_id, is_active, created_at)
                VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, TRUE, %s)
                ON CONFLICT(schedule_id, staff_id) DO UPDATE SET
                    start_session_id = COALESCE(EXCLUDED.start_session_id, schedule_staff.start_session_id),
                    end_session_id = EXCLUDED.end_session_id,
                    is_active = TRUE;
                """, (schedule_id, staff_id, start_session_id, end_session_id, now_iso))

            conn.commit()
            return staff_id
        except Exception as e:
            conn.rollback()
            logging.error(f"add_staff_member failed and rolled back: {e}")
            raise
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def get_staff_member(self, staff_id):
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM project_staff WHERE id = %s", (staff_id,))
            row = cursor.fetchone()
            return dict(row) if row else None
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def get_staff_by_code(self, project_id, staff_code):
        if not staff_code:
            return None
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute(
                "SELECT * FROM project_staff WHERE project_id = %s AND staff_code = %s",
                (project_id, str(staff_code).strip())
            )
            row = cursor.fetchone()
            return dict(row) if row else None
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def assign_staff_to_schedule(self, schedule_id, staff_id, start_session_id=None, end_session_id=None):
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("""
            SELECT s.project_id as sched_proj, ps.project_id as staff_proj
            FROM schedules s, project_staff ps
            WHERE s.id = %s AND ps.id = %s
            """, (schedule_id, staff_id))
            row = cursor.fetchone()
            if not row or row['sched_proj'] != row['staff_proj']:
                raise ValueError(
                    "ط¨ط±ظ†ط§ظ…ظ‡ ظˆ ظ†غŒط±ظˆ ظ…طھط¹ظ„ظ‚ ط¨ظ‡ غŒع© ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ غŒع©ط³ط§ظ† ظ†غŒط³طھظ†ط¯ ظˆ ط§ظ…ع©ط§ظ† ط§ظ†طھط³ط§ط¨ ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ ظ†ط¯ط§ط±ط¯."
                )

            now_iso = datetime.now()
            cursor.execute("""
            INSERT INTO schedule_staff (schedule_id, staff_id, start_session_id, end_session_id, is_active, created_at)
            VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, TRUE, %s)
            ON CONFLICT(schedule_id, staff_id) DO UPDATE SET
                start_session_id = COALESCE(EXCLUDED.start_session_id, schedule_staff.start_session_id),
                end_session_id = NULL,
                is_active = TRUE;
            """, (schedule_id, staff_id, start_session_id, end_session_id, now_iso))
            conn.commit()
            return True
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            raise
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def remove_staff_from_schedule(self, schedule_id, staff_id, end_session_id=None):
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("""
            UPDATE schedule_staff 
            SET is_active = FALSE, end_session_id = %s 
            WHERE schedule_id = %s AND staff_id = %s;
            """, (end_session_id, schedule_id, staff_id))
            conn.commit()
            return True
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            raise
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def list_schedule_staff(self, schedule_id, session_id=None, active_only=True):
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            query = """
            SELECT ps.*, ss.start_session_id as sched_start_session, ss.end_session_id as sched_end_session
            FROM schedule_staff ss
            JOIN project_staff ps ON ss.staff_id = ps.id
            WHERE ss.schedule_id = %s
            """
            params = [schedule_id]
            if active_only:
                query += " AND ss.is_active = TRUE AND ps.is_active = TRUE"
            if session_id is not None:
                query += " AND (ss.start_session_id IS NULL OR ss.start_session_id <= %s) AND (ss.end_session_id IS NULL OR ss.end_session_id >= %s)"
                params.extend([session_id, session_id])
            query += " ORDER BY ps.id ASC"
            cursor.execute(query, params)
            rows = cursor.fetchall()
            return [dict(r) for r in rows]
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def list_staff(self, project_id, unit=None, section=None, session_id=None, schedule_id=None, active_only=True):
        if schedule_id is not None:
            s_list = self.list_schedule_staff(
                schedule_id, session_id=session_id, active_only=active_only
            )
            if unit:
                s_list = [s for s in s_list if s.get('unit') == unit]
            if section:
                s_list = [s for s in s_list if s.get('section') == section]
            return s_list

        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            query = "SELECT * FROM project_staff WHERE project_id = %s"
            params = [project_id]
            if active_only:
                query += " AND is_active = TRUE"
            if unit:
                query += " AND unit = %s"
                params.append(unit)
            if section:
                query += " AND section = %s"
                params.append(section)
            if session_id is not None:
                query += " AND (start_session_id IS NULL OR start_session_id <= %s) AND (end_session_id IS NULL OR end_session_id >= %s)"
                params.extend([session_id, session_id])
            query += " ORDER BY id ASC"
            cursor.execute(query, params)
            rows = cursor.fetchall()
            return [dict(r) for r in rows]
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def search_staff(self, project_id, query_str, active_only=True, session_id=None, schedule_id=None):
        all_staff = self.list_staff(
            project_id, active_only=active_only, session_id=session_id, schedule_id=schedule_id
        )
        clean_q = normalize_persian(query_str)
        if not clean_q:
            return all_staff
        results = []
        for s in all_staff:
            name_norm = normalize_persian(s.get('name', ''))
            phone_norm = normalize_persian(s.get('phone', ''))
            unit_norm = normalize_persian(s.get('unit', ''))
            sec_norm = normalize_persian(s.get('section', ''))
            card_norm = normalize_persian(s.get('card_title', ''))
            code_norm = normalize_persian(s.get('staff_code', ''))
            if (clean_q in name_norm or clean_q in phone_norm or
                clean_q in unit_norm or clean_q in sec_norm or clean_q in card_norm or
                    clean_q in code_norm):
                results.append(s)
        return results

    def get_staff_by_phone_group(self, project_id):
        all_staff = self.list_staff(project_id, active_only=False)
        mapping = {}
        for s in all_staff:
            p = str(s.get('phone', '')).strip()
            if p and p not in ('None', '-', ''):
                if p not in mapping:
                    mapping[p] = []
                mapping[p].append(s)
        return mapping

    def update_staff_field(self, staff_id, field_name, value, sync_same_phone=True):
        staff = self.get_staff_member(staff_id)
        if not staff:
            return False
        allowed_fields = [
            'name', 'phone', 'unit', 'section', 'position', 'card_title',
            'shift_time', 'gender', 'notes', 'is_active', 'is_multi_section',
            'staff_code', 'start_session_id', 'end_session_id', '_excel_row'
        ]
        if field_name not in allowed_fields:
            return False
        project_id = staff['project_id']
        phone = str(staff.get('phone', '')).strip()
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            if sync_same_phone and phone and phone not in ('None', '-', '') and field_name in ['name', 'phone', 'gender']:
                cursor.execute(
                    f"UPDATE project_staff SET {field_name} = %s WHERE project_id = %s AND phone = %s",
                    (value, project_id, phone)
                )
            else:
                cursor.execute(
                    f"UPDATE project_staff SET {field_name} = %s WHERE id = %s",
                    (value, staff_id)
                )
            conn.commit()
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            conn.rollback()
            logging.error(f"update_staff_field error: {e}")
            return False
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def update_section_shift_time(self, project_id, unit, section, new_time):
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("""
            UPDATE project_staff 
            SET shift_time = %s 
            WHERE project_id = %s AND unit = %s AND section = %s;
            """, (new_time.strip(), project_id, unit.strip(), section.strip()))
            conn.commit()
            return True
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            return False
        finally:
            conn.close()
