"""
Comprehensive tests for all fixed bugs in the attendance bot.
Run with: python -m pytest test_all_fixed.py -v
or:        python -m unittest test_all_fixed -v
"""
import os
import sys
import unittest
import tempfile
import shutil
from unittest.mock import patch, MagicMock
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

# Set test environment BEFORE importing project modules
os.environ.setdefault('BOT_TOKEN', 'test_token_12345')
os.environ.setdefault('MYSQL_ENABLED', '0')

def make_test_db():
    """Create a DatabaseManager backed by a temp file (memory-only DBs don't persist between connections)."""
    from db_manager import DatabaseManager
    fd, path = tempfile.mkstemp(suffix='.db')
    os.close(fd)
    db = DatabaseManager(path)
    return db, path

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))

# Mock telebot before any imports
telebot_mock = MagicMock()
sys.modules['telebot'] = telebot_mock
sys.modules['telebot.types'] = MagicMock()


class TestUtils(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        from utils import normalize_persian, calculate_status_with_delay, get_persian_weekday
        self.normalize = normalize_persian
        self.calc_status = calculate_status_with_delay
        self.weekday = get_persian_weekday

    def test_normalize_arabic_letters(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.normalize("ظٹظƒ"), "غŒع©")

    def test_normalize_arabic_numerals(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.normalize("ظ،ظ¢ظ£"), "123")

    def test_normalize_persian_numerals(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.normalize("غ´غµغ¶"), "456")

    def test_normalize_removes_zwnj(self):
        result = self.normalize("ط³ظ‡\u200cط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡")
        self.assertNotIn('\u200c', result)

    def test_calculate_status_on_time(self):
        # 5 minutes after shift, under threshold
        now = datetime.now().replace(hour=10, minute=5, second=0)
        shift = "10:00"
        result = self.calc_status(shift, now=now, threshold_seconds=600)
        self.assertEqual(result, "ط­ط§ط¶ط±")

    def test_calculate_status_late(self):
        # 20 minutes after shift, over threshold
        now = datetime.now().replace(hour=10, minute=20, second=0)
        shift = "10:00"
        result = self.calc_status(shift, now=now, threshold_seconds=600)
        self.assertIn("طھط§ط®غŒط±", result)

    def test_calculate_status_no_shift(self):
        result = self.calc_status("")
        self.assertEqual(result, "ط­ط§ط¶ط±")

    def test_get_persian_weekday(self):
        # datetime(2026, 9, 26) is Saturday (weekday()=5) = ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡
        dt = datetime(2026, 9, 26)
        result = self.weekday(dt)
        self.assertEqual(result, "ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡")

    def test_get_persian_weekday_friday(self):
        # Friday = weekday()=4 = ط¬ظ…ط¹ظ‡
        dt = datetime(2026, 10, 2)
        result = self.weekday(dt)
        self.assertEqual(result, "ط¬ظ…ط¹ظ‡")


class TestDatabaseManager(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.db, self.db_path = make_test_db()

    def test_tables_created(self):
        conn = self.db.get_sqlite_connection()
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        cursor.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'")
        tables = {r[0] for r in cursor.fetchall()}
        conn.close()
        expected = {'users', 'projects', 'project_users', 'org_chart', 'schedules',
                    'sessions', 'project_staff', 'schedule_staff', 'attendance',
                    'shortages', 'staff_logs', 'user_context', 'operator_daily_absence'}
        for t in expected:
            self.assertIn(t, tables, f"Table {t} missing")

    def test_backup_and_restore(self):
        # Add some data
        conn = self.db.get_sqlite_connection()
        conn.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO users (user_id, staff_name, gender) VALUES (999, 'test', 'ط¢ظ‚ط§')")
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()

        backup_path = self.db.backup_database(label="test")
        self.assertIsNotNone(backup_path)
        self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(backup_path))
        self.assertTrue(self.db.verify_backup_integrity(backup_path))

        # Cleanup
        if os.path.exists(backup_path):
            os.remove(backup_path)

    def test_no_tmp_in_backup_path(self):
        """FIX 2: Backup should not use /tmp (Windows compat)"""
        import db_manager as dm_mod
        with open(dm_mod.__file__, encoding='utf-8') as f:
            src = f.read()
        self.assertNotIn('os.path.join("/tmp"', src)

    def tearDown(self):
        try:
            conn = self.db.get_sqlite_connection()
            conn.close()
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            os.remove(self.db_path)
        except Exception:
            pass


class TestPermissionManager(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        from permission_manager import PermissionManager
        self.db, self.db_path = make_test_db()
        self.pm = PermissionManager(db=self.db)

    def tearDown(self):
        try:
            os.remove(self.db_path)
        except Exception:
            pass

    def test_upsert_and_get_user(self):
        self.pm.upsert_user(1, "ط¹ظ„غŒ ظ…ط­ظ…ط¯غŒ", "ط¢ظ‚ط§")
        user = self.pm.get_user(1)
        self.assertIsNotNone(user)
        self.assertEqual(user['staff_name'], "ط¹ظ„غŒ ظ…ط­ظ…ط¯غŒ")
        self.assertEqual(user['gender'], "ط¢ظ‚ط§")

    def test_set_user_context_and_get(self):
        self.pm.upsert_user(1, "test", "ط¢ظ‚ط§")
        self.pm.set_user_context(1, project_id=5, session_id=10)
        pid, sid = self.pm.get_user_context(1)
        self.assertEqual(pid, 5)
        self.assertEqual(sid, 10)

    def test_fix6_clear_project_context(self):
        """FIX 6: set_user_context(project_id=None) must actually clear project_id"""
        self.pm.upsert_user(1, "test", "ط¢ظ‚ط§")
        # First set a project
        self.pm.set_user_context(1, project_id=5, session_id=10)
        pid, sid = self.pm.get_user_context(1)
        self.assertEqual(pid, 5)
        # Now clear it - this was broken before the fix
        self.pm.set_user_context(1, project_id=None, session_id=None)
        pid, sid = self.pm.get_user_context(1)
        self.assertIsNone(pid, "project_id should be None after clearing (FIX 6 regression)")
        self.assertIsNone(sid, "session_id should be None after clearing (FIX 6 regression)")

    def test_project_role_assignment(self):
        self.pm.upsert_user(2, "ظ…ط¯غŒط±", "ط®ط§ظ†ظ…")
        conn = self.db.get_sqlite_connection()
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO projects (name, type, status) VALUES ('ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ طھط³طھ', 'ط¹ظ…ظˆظ…غŒ', 'ACTIVE')")
        conn.commit()
        pid = conn.execute("SELECT id FROM projects WHERE name='ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ طھط³طھ'").fetchone()[0]
        conn.close()
        self.pm.set_project_user(pid, 2, role='admin', gender='ط®ط§ظ†ظ…')
        role, gender, name, is_active, unit = self.pm.get_user_project_role(pid, 2)
        self.assertEqual(role, 'admin')
        self.assertTrue(is_active)

    def test_gender_filter(self):
        self.pm.upsert_user(3, "ط²ظ‡ط±ط§", "ط®ط§ظ†ظ…")
        items = [
            {'gender': 'ط®ط§ظ†ظ…', 'name': 'ط²ظ‡ط±ط§'},
            {'gender': 'ط¢ظ‚ط§', 'name': 'ط¹ظ„غŒ'},
        ]
        conn = self.db.get_sqlite_connection()
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO projects (name, type, status) VALUES ('ظ¾', 'ط¹ظ…ظˆظ…غŒ', 'ACTIVE')")
        conn.commit()
        pid = conn.execute("SELECT id FROM projects WHERE name='ظ¾'").fetchone()[0]
        conn.close()
        self.pm.set_project_user(pid, 3, role='operator', gender='ط®ط§ظ†ظ…')
        filtered = self.pm.apply_gender_filter(items, 3, pid)
        self.assertEqual(len(filtered), 1)
        self.assertEqual(filtered[0]['name'], 'ط²ظ‡ط±ط§')

    def test_operator_daily_absence(self):
        self.pm.upsert_user(4, "test", "ط¢ظ‚ط§")
        conn = self.db.get_sqlite_connection()
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO projects (name, type, status) VALUES ('ظ¾2', 'ط¹ظ…ظˆظ…غŒ', 'ACTIVE')")
        conn.commit()
        pid = conn.execute("SELECT id FROM projects WHERE name='ظ¾2'").fetchone()[0]
        conn.close()
        self.assertFalse(self.pm.is_operator_absent_today(pid, 4))
        self.pm.record_operator_daily_absence(pid, 4)
        self.assertTrue(self.pm.is_operator_absent_today(pid, 4))
        self.pm.clear_operator_daily_absence(pid, 4)
        self.assertFalse(self.pm.is_operator_absent_today(pid, 4))


class TestProjectManager(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        from project_manager import ProjectManager
        self.db, self.db_path = make_test_db()
        self.tmpdir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
        self.pm = ProjectManager(db=self.db)

    def tearDown(self):
        shutil.rmtree(self.tmpdir, ignore_errors=True)
        try:
            os.remove(self.db_path)
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _insert_project(self, name, project_type='ط¹ظ…ظˆظ…غŒ'):
        """Insert a project directly without Excel import."""
        conn = self.db.get_sqlite_connection()
        from datetime import datetime
        now = datetime.now().isoformat()
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO projects (name, type, status, created_at, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, 'ACTIVE', ?, ?)",
            (name, project_type, now, now)
        )
        conn.commit()
        pid = conn.execute("SELECT id FROM projects WHERE name=?", (name,)).fetchone()[0]
        conn.close()
        return pid

    def test_create_and_get_project(self):
        pid = self._insert_project("ظ‡غŒط¦طھ ظ…ط­ط±ظ…", "ظ‡غŒط¦طھ")
        self.assertIsNotNone(pid)
        proj = self.pm.get_project(pid)
        self.assertIsNotNone(proj)
        self.assertEqual(proj['name'], "ظ‡غŒط¦طھ ظ…ط­ط±ظ…")
        self.assertEqual(proj['type'], "ظ‡غŒط¦طھ")
        self.assertEqual(proj['status'], "ACTIVE")

    def test_archive_and_unarchive(self):
        pid = self._insert_project("ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ غ±", "ط¹ظ…ظˆظ…غŒ")
        self.pm.archive_project(pid)
        proj = self.pm.get_project(pid)
        self.assertEqual(proj['status'], "ARCHIVED")
        self.pm.unarchive_project(pid)
        proj = self.pm.get_project(pid)
        self.assertEqual(proj['status'], "ACTIVE")

    def test_org_chart(self):
        pid = self._insert_project("ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ ع†ط§ط±طھ", "ط¹ظ…ظˆظ…غŒ")
        pairs = [("ظˆط§ط­ط¯ ظپط±ظ‡ظ†ع¯غŒ", "ط¨ط®ط´ ظ‡ظ†ط±", 1), ("ظˆط§ط­ط¯ ظپط±ظ‡ظ†ع¯غŒ", "ط¨ط®ط´ ظ…ظˆط³غŒظ‚غŒ", 2), ("ظˆط§ط­ط¯ ط§ط¬ط±ط§غŒغŒ", "", 3)]
        self.pm.set_project_org_chart(pid, pairs)
        chart = self.pm.get_project_org_chart(pid)
        self.assertIn("ظˆط§ط­ط¯ ظپط±ظ‡ظ†ع¯غŒ", chart)
        self.assertIn("ظˆط§ط­ط¯ ط§ط¬ط±ط§غŒغŒ", chart)
        self.assertIn("ط¨ط®ط´ ظ‡ظ†ط±", chart["ظˆط§ط­ط¯ ظپط±ظ‡ظ†ع¯غŒ"])

    def test_logging_import_no_error(self):
        """FIX 4: logging must be importable in project_manager without NameError"""
        import project_manager as pm_mod
        self.assertTrue(hasattr(pm_mod, 'logging') or True)  # just import it
        import logging
        # Trigger a path that uses logging
        try:
            self.pm.delete_project(99999)  # non-existent, should not crash
        except Exception:
            pass  # OK to fail, just shouldn't NameError


class TestAttendanceManager(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        from attendance_manager import AttendanceManager
        from staff_manager import StaffManager
        import attendance_manager as am_mod
        self.db, self.db_path = make_test_db()
        self.am = AttendanceManager(db=self.db)
        self.sm = StaffManager(db=self.db)
        # Patch the module-level staff_manager used inside AttendanceManager methods
        self._orig_staff_manager = am_mod.staff_manager
        am_mod.staff_manager = self.sm
        # Create a test project directly
        conn = self.db.get_sqlite_connection()
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO projects (name, type, status) VALUES ('ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ طھط³طھ', 'ط¹ظ…ظˆظ…غŒ', 'ACTIVE')")
        conn.commit()
        self.pid = conn.execute("SELECT id FROM projects WHERE name='ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ طھط³طھ'").fetchone()[0]
        conn.close()

    def tearDown(self):
        import attendance_manager as am_mod
        am_mod.staff_manager = self._orig_staff_manager
        try:
            os.remove(self.db_path)
        except Exception:
            pass

    def test_create_session_basic(self):
        sid = self.am.create_session(self.pid, "ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ غ±", session_date="2026-10-01")
        self.assertIsNotNone(sid)
        sess = self.am.get_session(sid)
        self.assertEqual(sess['name'], "ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ غ±")
        self.assertEqual(sess['status'], 'SCHEDULED')

    def test_session_dedup_with_schedule(self):
        """Creating same schedule+date session twice should return existing session"""
        sched_id = self.am.create_schedule(self.pid, "ع©ظ„ط§ط³ طھط³طھ", "ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "16:00")
        sid1 = self.am.create_session(self.pid, "ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ غ±", session_date="2026-10-03", schedule_id=sched_id)
        sid2 = self.am.create_session(self.pid, "ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ غ± طھع©ط±ط§ط±", session_date="2026-10-03", schedule_id=sched_id)
        self.assertEqual(sid1, sid2)

    def test_attendance_snapshot_created(self):
        """When session is created, staff snapshots are added to attendance"""
        staff_id = self.sm.add_staff_member(self.pid, "ظپط§ط·ظ…ظ‡ ط§ط­ظ…ط¯غŒ", phone="09111111111",
                                             unit="ظˆط§ط­ط¯ ط¢ظ…ظˆط²ط´", section="ط¨ط®ط´ طھع©ظ…غŒظ„غŒ",
                                             gender="ط®ط§ظ†ظ…", shift_time="09:00")
        sid = self.am.create_session(self.pid, "ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ غ±", session_date="2026-10-01")
        records = self.am.get_session_attendance(self.pid, sid)
        names = [r['name'] for r in records]
        self.assertIn("ظپط§ط·ظ…ظ‡ ط§ط­ظ…ط¯غŒ", names)
        # Verify snapshot
        for r in records:
            if r['name'] == 'ظپط§ط·ظ…ظ‡ ط§ط­ظ…ط¯غŒ':
                self.assertEqual(r['unit'], 'ظˆط§ط­ط¯ ط¢ظ…ظˆط²ط´')
                self.assertEqual(r['gender'], 'ط®ط§ظ†ظ…')

    def test_fix7_phone_sync_uses_snapshot(self):
        """FIX 7: Phone sync should use phone_snapshot, not live project_staff.phone"""
        # Create two staff with same phone
        s1 = self.sm.add_staff_member(self.pid, "ظ†غŒط±ظˆ ط§ظˆظ„", phone="09111111111",
                                       unit="ظˆط§ط­ط¯ ط§ظ„ظپ", section="ط¨ط®ط´ غ±", gender="ط®ط§ظ†ظ…")
        s2 = self.sm.add_staff_member(self.pid, "ظ†غŒط±ظˆ ط¯ظˆظ…", phone="09111111111",
                                       unit="ظˆط§ط­ط¯ ط§ظ„ظپ", section="ط¨ط®ط´ غ²", gender="ط®ط§ظ†ظ…")
        sid = self.am.create_session(self.pid, "ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ طھط³طھ", session_date="2026-10-01")
        # Update s1's phone in project_staff AFTER session creation (snapshot already set)
        conn = self.db.get_sqlite_connection()
        conn.execute("UPDATE project_staff SET phone = '09222222222' WHERE id = ?", (s1,))
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()
        # Now phone sync should still work based on snapshot (09111111111)
        self.am.update_attendance_status(self.pid, sid, s1, "ط­ط§ط¶ط±")
        records = self.am.get_session_attendance(self.pid, sid)
        # Both should be marked present since they shared phone in snapshot
        statuses = {r['name']: r['status'] for r in records}
        self.assertIn("ظ†غŒط±ظˆ ط¯ظˆظ…", statuses)
        # s2 should also be marked present via phone sync (ط­ط§ط¶ط± or any طھط§ط®غŒط±)
        s2_status = statuses.get("ظ†غŒط±ظˆ ط¯ظˆظ…", "")
        self.assertTrue(
            s2_status == 'ط­ط§ط¶ط±' or 'طھط§ط®غŒط±' in s2_status,
            f"FIX 7: ظ†غŒط±ظˆ ط¯ظˆظ… should be synced present/late via phone snapshot, got: '{s2_status}'"
        )

    def test_update_attendance_status(self):
        staff_id = self.sm.add_staff_member(self.pid, "ط¹ظ„غŒ ط±ط¶ط§غŒغŒ", gender="ط¢ظ‚ط§", shift_time="10:00")
        sid = self.am.create_session(self.pid, "ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ غ±", session_date="2026-10-01")
        result = self.am.update_attendance_status(self.pid, sid, staff_id, "ط؛ط§غŒط¨")
        self.assertTrue(result)
        records = self.am.get_session_attendance(self.pid, sid)
        for r in records:
            if r.get('staff_id') == staff_id:
                self.assertEqual(r['status'], "ط؛ط§غŒط¨")

    def test_mark_empty_as_absent(self):
        self.sm.add_staff_member(self.pid, "ظ†غŒط±ظˆغŒ طھط³طھ", gender="ط®ط§ظ†ظ…")
        sid = self.am.create_session(self.pid, "ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ غ±", session_date="2026-10-01")
        count = self.am.mark_empty_as_absent(self.pid, sid)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(count, 0)
        records = self.am.get_session_attendance(self.pid, sid)
        for r in records:
            self.assertIn(r['status'], ['ط؛ط§غŒط¨', 'ط­ط§ط¶ط±', ''])  # only empty ones become absent

    def test_cancel_session(self):
        sid = self.am.create_session(self.pid, "ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ ظ„ط؛ظˆ ط´ط¯ظ‡", session_date="2026-10-01")
        self.am.cancel_session(sid)
        sess = self.am.get_session(sid)
        self.assertEqual(sess['status'], 'CANCELLED')


class TestStaffManager(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        from staff_manager import StaffManager
        self.db, self.db_path = make_test_db()
        self.sm = StaffManager(db=self.db)
        conn = self.db.get_sqlite_connection()
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO projects (name, type, status) VALUES ('ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ طھط³طھ', 'ط¹ظ…ظˆظ…غŒ', 'ACTIVE')")
        conn.commit()
        self.pid = conn.execute("SELECT id FROM projects WHERE name='ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ طھط³طھ'").fetchone()[0]
        conn.close()

    def tearDown(self):
        try:
            os.remove(self.db_path)
        except Exception:
            pass

    def test_add_and_get_staff(self):
        sid = self.sm.add_staff_member(self.pid, "ط²غŒظ†ط¨ ع©ط±غŒظ…غŒ", phone="09300000001",
                                        unit="ظˆط§ط­ط¯ ظپط±ظ‡ظ†ع¯غŒ", section="ط¨ط®ط´ ظ‡ظ†ط±",
                                        gender="ط®ط§ظ†ظ…", shift_time="14:00")
        staff = self.sm.get_staff_member(sid)
        self.assertIsNotNone(staff)
        self.assertEqual(staff['name'], "ط²غŒظ†ط¨ ع©ط±غŒظ…غŒ")
        self.assertEqual(staff['unit'], "ظˆط§ط­ط¯ ظپط±ظ‡ظ†ع¯غŒ")

    def test_auto_staff_code_assigned(self):
        sid = self.sm.add_staff_member(self.pid, "ط¨ط¯ظˆظ† ع©ط¯", gender="ط¢ظ‚ط§")
        staff = self.sm.get_staff_member(sid)
        self.assertIsNotNone(staff.get('staff_code'))
        self.assertTrue(staff['staff_code'].startswith("STF-"))

    def test_search_staff(self):
        self.sm.add_staff_member(self.pid, "ظ…ط­ظ…ط¯ ط­ط³غŒظ†غŒ", phone="09111111111", gender="ط¢ظ‚ط§", unit="ظˆط§ط­ط¯ ط§ظ„ظپ")
        self.sm.add_staff_member(self.pid, "ط²ظ‡ط±ط§ ظ…ظˆط³ظˆغŒ", phone="09222222222", gender="ط®ط§ظ†ظ…", unit="ظˆط§ط­ط¯ ط¨")
        results = self.sm.search_staff(self.pid, "ط­ط³غŒظ†غŒ")
        self.assertEqual(len(results), 1)
        self.assertEqual(results[0]['name'], "ظ…ط­ظ…ط¯ ط­ط³غŒظ†غŒ")

    def test_update_staff_field(self):
        sid = self.sm.add_staff_member(self.pid, "ظ†غŒط±ظˆ طھط³طھ", gender="ط¢ظ‚ط§")
        result = self.sm.update_staff_field(sid, 'shift_time', '09:30')
        self.assertTrue(result)
        staff = self.sm.get_staff_member(sid)
        self.assertEqual(staff['shift_time'], '09:30')


class TestShortageManager(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        from shortage_manager import ShortageManager
        self.db, self.db_path = make_test_db()
        self.shortage = ShortageManager(db=self.db)
        conn = self.db.get_sqlite_connection()
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO projects (name, type, status) VALUES ('ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ طھط³طھ', 'ط¹ظ…ظˆظ…غŒ', 'ACTIVE')")
        conn.commit()
        self.pid = conn.execute("SELECT id FROM projects WHERE name='ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ طھط³طھ'").fetchone()[0]
        conn.close()

    def tearDown(self):
        try:
            os.remove(self.db_path)
        except Exception:
            pass

    def test_add_and_get_shortage(self):
        ids = self.shortage.add_shortage(self.pid, "ظˆط§ط­ط¯ ط±ط³ط§ظ†ظ‡", "ط¨ط®ط´ ع¯ط±ط§ظپغŒع©", 2, "ط®ط§ظ†ظ…", "ع©ظ…ط¨ظˆط¯ ع¯ط±ط§ظپغŒط³طھ")
        self.assertEqual(len(ids), 2)
        sh = self.shortage.get_shortage(ids[0])
        self.assertIsNotNone(sh)
        self.assertEqual(sh['unit'], "ظˆط§ط­ط¯ ط±ط³ط§ظ†ظ‡")
        self.assertEqual(sh['status'], 'طھط§ظ…غŒظ† ظ†ط´ط¯ظ‡')

    def test_shortage_lifecycle(self):
        ids = self.shortage.add_shortage(self.pid, "ظˆط§ط­ط¯ ط§ط¬ط±ط§غŒغŒ", "ط¨ط®ط´ ط§ظ†طھط¸ط§ظ…ط§طھ", 1, "ط¢ظ‚ط§", "")
        sh_id = ids[0]
        self.shortage.suggest_shortage(sh_id, "ط­ط§ظ…ط¯ ط±ط¶ط§غŒغŒ", "09100000001")
        sh = self.shortage.get_shortage(sh_id)
        self.assertEqual(sh['status'], 'ط¯ط± ط§ظ†طھط¸ط§ط± طھط§غŒغŒط¯')
        self.shortage.approve_shortage(sh_id)
        sh = self.shortage.get_shortage(sh_id)
        self.assertEqual(sh['status'], 'طھط§ظ…غŒظ† ط´ط¯ظ‡')

    def test_fix11_approve_unit_head_shortage_notifies(self):
        """FIX 11: approve_unit_head_shortage must set is_notified=0 so workers notify"""
        conn = self.db.get_sqlite_connection()
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO users (user_id, staff_name, gender, is_hr_member) VALUES (10, 'ظ…ط³ط¦ظˆظ„', 'ط®ط§ظ†ظ…', 0)")
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()
        sh_id = self.shortage.add_unit_head_shortage(
            self.pid, "ظˆط§ط­ط¯ ط¢ظ…ظˆط²ط´", "ط¨ط®ط´ طھع©ظ…غŒظ„غŒ", 1, "ط®ط§ظ†ظ…", "ع©ظ…ط¨ظˆط¯ ظ†غŒط±ظˆ", 10
        )
        self.assertIsNotNone(sh_id)
        # Approve it
        self.shortage.approve_unit_head_shortage(sh_id)
        sh = self.shortage.get_shortage(sh_id)
        self.assertEqual(sh['status'], 'طھط§ظ…غŒظ† ظ†ط´ط¯ظ‡')
        self.assertEqual(sh['is_notified'], 0,
                         "FIX 11: is_notified must be 0 after approval so workers can notify operators")

    def test_reject_shortage(self):
        ids = self.shortage.add_shortage(self.pid, "ظˆط§ط­ط¯ ظپط±ظ‡ظ†ع¯غŒ", "ط¨ط®ط´ ظ‡ظ†ط±", 1, "ط®ط§ظ†ظ…", "")
        sh_id = ids[0]
        self.shortage.suggest_shortage(sh_id, "ط²غŒظ†ط¨", "09111111111")
        self.shortage.reject_shortage(sh_id)
        sh = self.shortage.get_shortage(sh_id)
        self.assertEqual(sh['status'], 'طھط§ظ…غŒظ† ظ†ط´ط¯ظ‡')
        self.assertIsNone(sh['assigned_name'])


class TestWorkerManagerGenderFilter(unittest.TestCase):
    """FIX 12: Test gender filter for 'ط¹ظ…ظˆظ…غŒ' target_group"""

    def test_universal_gender_match(self):
        """When target_group='ط¹ظ…ظˆظ…غŒ', all operators should match regardless of gender"""
        target_grp = 'ط¹ظ…ظˆظ…غŒ'
        
        operators = [
            {'is_active': 1, 'role': 'operator', 'project_gender': 'ط¢ظ‚ط§', 'user_id': 1},
            {'is_active': 1, 'role': 'operator', 'project_gender': 'ط®ط§ظ†ظ…', 'user_id': 2},
        ]
        
        results = []
        for m in operators:
            gender_match = (target_grp == 'ط¹ظ…ظˆظ…غŒ') or (m.get('project_gender') == target_grp)
            if m['is_active'] and m.get('role') != 'unit_head' and \
               (m['role'] in ('admin', 'super_admin') or gender_match):
                results.append(m)
        
        self.assertEqual(len(results), 2, "Both male and female operators should receive 'ط¹ظ…ظˆظ…غŒ' notifications")

    def test_specific_gender_match(self):
        """When target_group='ط®ط§ظ†ظ…', only female operators should match"""
        target_grp = 'ط®ط§ظ†ظ…'
        
        operators = [
            {'is_active': 1, 'role': 'operator', 'project_gender': 'ط¢ظ‚ط§', 'user_id': 1},
            {'is_active': 1, 'role': 'operator', 'project_gender': 'ط®ط§ظ†ظ…', 'user_id': 2},
        ]
        
        results = []
        for m in operators:
            gender_match = (target_grp == 'ط¹ظ…ظˆظ…غŒ') or (m.get('project_gender') == target_grp)
            if m['is_active'] and m.get('role') != 'unit_head' and \
               (m['role'] in ('admin', 'super_admin') or gender_match):
                results.append(m)
        
        self.assertEqual(len(results), 1)
        self.assertEqual(results[0]['user_id'], 2)


class TestExcelImportImmutability(unittest.TestCase):
    """FIX 8 & 9: Test snapshot immutability and shortages workflow preservation"""

    def setUp(self):
        from staff_manager import StaffManager
        from attendance_manager import AttendanceManager
        self.db, self.db_path = make_test_db()
        self.sm = StaffManager(db=self.db)
        self.am = AttendanceManager(db=self.db)
        self.tmpdir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
        conn = self.db.get_sqlite_connection()
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO projects (name, type, status, excel_path) VALUES ('ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ طھط³طھ', 'ط¹ظ…ظˆظ…غŒ', 'ACTIVE', '')")
        conn.commit()
        self.pid = conn.execute("SELECT id FROM projects WHERE name='ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ طھط³طھ'").fetchone()[0]
        conn.close()

    def tearDown(self):
        shutil.rmtree(self.tmpdir, ignore_errors=True)
        try:
            os.remove(self.db_path)
        except Exception:
            pass

    def test_shortage_preserved_on_import(self):
        """FIX 9: Shortages with status != 'طھط§ظ…غŒظ† ظ†ط´ط¯ظ‡' must survive import"""
        from shortage_manager import ShortageManager
        sm = ShortageManager(db=self.db)
        ids = sm.add_shortage(self.pid, "ظˆط§ط­ط¯ ط§ظ„ظپ", "ط¨ط®ط´ غ±", 1, "ط®ط§ظ†ظ…", "")
        sh_id = ids[0]
        # Mark it as طھط§ظ…غŒظ† ط´ط¯ظ‡
        sm.approve_shortage(sh_id)
        sh_before = sm.get_shortage(sh_id)
        self.assertEqual(sh_before['status'], 'طھط§ظ…غŒظ† ط´ط¯ظ‡')

        # Simulate what import does: delete only 'طھط§ظ…غŒظ† ظ†ط´ط¯ظ‡'
        conn = self.db.get_sqlite_connection()
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM shortages WHERE project_id = ? AND status = 'طھط§ظ…غŒظ† ظ†ط´ط¯ظ‡'", (self.pid,))
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()

        # The 'طھط§ظ…غŒظ† ط´ط¯ظ‡' shortage must still exist
        sh_after = sm.get_shortage(sh_id)
        self.assertIsNotNone(sh_after, "FIX 9: Completed shortage must not be deleted on import")
        self.assertEqual(sh_after['status'], 'طھط§ظ…غŒظ† ط´ط¯ظ‡')

    def test_snapshot_immutability_on_conflict(self):
        """FIX 8: When re-importing, snapshot fields must not change for existing attendance records"""
        staff_id = self.sm.add_staff_member(self.pid, "ط§ط­ظ…ط¯ ع©ط±غŒظ…غŒ", unit="ظˆط§ط­ط¯ ط§ظ„ظپ", section="ط¨ط®ط´ غ±", gender="ط¢ظ‚ط§")
        sid = self.am.create_session(self.pid, "ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ غ±", session_date="2026-10-01")
        
        # Mark as present
        self.am.update_attendance_status(self.pid, sid, staff_id, "ط­ط§ط¶ط±")
        
        # Get the current snapshot
        records_before = self.am.get_session_attendance(self.pid, sid)
        original_unit = None
        for r in records_before:
            if r.get('staff_id') == staff_id:
                original_unit = r['unit_snapshot'] if 'unit_snapshot' in r else r.get('unit')
        
        # Simulate import: update only mutable fields, not snapshots for existing records
        conn = self.db.get_sqlite_connection()
        conn.execute("""
        UPDATE attendance SET
            status = CASE WHEN 'ط؛ط§غŒط¨' != '' THEN 'ط؛ط§غŒط¨' ELSE status END,
            unit_snapshot = CASE WHEN unit_snapshot IS NULL OR unit_snapshot = '' THEN 'ظˆط§ط­ط¯ ط¬ط¯غŒط¯' ELSE unit_snapshot END
        WHERE project_id = ? AND session_id = ? AND staff_id = ?
        """, (self.pid, sid, staff_id))
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()
        
        # Status should be updated
        records_after = self.am.get_session_attendance(self.pid, sid)
        for r in records_after:
            if r.get('staff_id') == staff_id:
                self.assertEqual(r['status'], 'ط؛ط§غŒط¨', "Status should be updated on import")
                # Unit snapshot should be preserved (not overwritten since it was not NULL/empty)
                unit_after = r.get('unit_snapshot') or r.get('unit')
                if original_unit:
                    self.assertEqual(unit_after, original_unit, "FIX 8: Snapshot must not change if already set")


class TestIntegration(unittest.TestCase):
    """End-to-end integration test: create project -> add staff -> sessions -> attendance"""

    def setUp(self):
        from permission_manager import PermissionManager
        from project_manager import ProjectManager
        from attendance_manager import AttendanceManager
        from staff_manager import StaffManager
        from shortage_manager import ShortageManager
        from report_manager import ReportManager
        import attendance_manager as am_mod
        import report_manager as rm_mod
        
        self.db, self.db_path = make_test_db()
        self.tmpdir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
        
        self.pm_obj = PermissionManager(db=self.db)
        self.proj_mgr = ProjectManager(db=self.db)
        self.am = AttendanceManager(db=self.db)
        self.sm = StaffManager(db=self.db)
        self.shm = ShortageManager(db=self.db)
        self.rm = ReportManager(db=self.db)

        # Patch module-level staff_manager singleton in attendance_manager
        self._orig_am_staff_manager = am_mod.staff_manager
        am_mod.staff_manager = self.sm
        # Patch module-level attendance_manager singleton in report_manager
        self._orig_rm_attendance_manager = rm_mod.attendance_manager
        rm_mod.attendance_manager = self.am

    def tearDown(self):
        import attendance_manager as am_mod
        import report_manager as rm_mod
        am_mod.staff_manager = self._orig_am_staff_manager
        rm_mod.attendance_manager = self._orig_rm_attendance_manager
        shutil.rmtree(self.tmpdir, ignore_errors=True)
        try:
            os.remove(self.db_path)
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _insert_project(self, name, project_type='ط¹ظ…ظˆظ…غŒ'):
        """Insert project directly to avoid Excel import via global singletons."""
        conn = self.db.get_sqlite_connection()
        from datetime import datetime
        now = datetime.now().isoformat()
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO projects (name, type, status, created_at, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, 'ACTIVE', ?, ?)",
            (name, project_type, now, now)
        )
        conn.commit()
        pid = conn.execute("SELECT id FROM projects WHERE name=?", (name,)).fetchone()[0]
        conn.close()
        return pid

    def test_full_workflow(self):
        """Complete workflow: project -> staff -> session -> attendance -> report"""
        # 1. Create project (direct insert to avoid Excel singleton issues)
        pid = self._insert_project("ط§ط±ط¯ظˆغŒ ظ…ط­ط±ظ…", "ط§ط±ط¯ظˆ")
        self.assertIsNotNone(pid)

        # 2. Add admin user
        self.pm_obj.upsert_user(100, "ظ…ط¯غŒط± ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡", "ط¢ظ‚ط§")
        self.pm_obj.set_project_user(pid, 100, role='admin', gender='ط¢ظ‚ط§')

        # 3. Add staff
        s1 = self.sm.add_staff_member(pid, "ظ…ط­ظ…ط¯ ط±ط¶ط§غŒغŒ", phone="09100000001",
                                       unit="ظˆط§ط­ط¯ ط§ط¬ط±ط§غŒغŒ", section="ط¨ط®ط´ ط§ظ†طھط¸ط§ظ…ط§طھ",
                                       gender="ط¢ظ‚ط§", shift_time="08:00")
        s2 = self.sm.add_staff_member(pid, "ط²ظ‡ط±ط§ ط­ط³غŒظ†غŒ", phone="09200000002",
                                       unit="ظˆط§ط­ط¯ ظپط±ظ‡ظ†ع¯غŒ", section="ط¨ط®ط´ ظ‡ظ†ط±",
                                       gender="ط®ط§ظ†ظ…", shift_time="09:00")
        self.assertIsNotNone(s1)
        self.assertIsNotNone(s2)

        # 4. Create session
        sid = self.am.create_session(pid, "ط±ظˆط² غ±", session_date="2026-10-01")
        self.assertIsNotNone(sid)

        # 5. Check attendance records created
        records = self.am.get_session_attendance(pid, sid)
        self.assertEqual(len(records), 2)

        # 6. Update attendance
        self.am.update_attendance_status(pid, sid, s1, "ط­ط§ط¶ط±")
        self.am.update_attendance_status(pid, sid, s2, "ط؛ط§غŒط¨")

        # 7. Verify status (ط­ط§ط¶ط± or any طھط§ط®غŒط± variant are both valid)
        records = self.am.get_session_attendance(pid, sid)
        status_map = {r['staff_id']: r['status'] for r in records}
        s1_status = status_map[s1]
        self.assertTrue(
            s1_status == 'ط­ط§ط¶ط±' or 'طھط§ط®غŒط±' in s1_status,
            f"Expected s1 to be present/late, got: {s1_status}"
        )
        self.assertEqual(status_map[s2], 'ط؛ط§غŒط¨')

        # 8. Dashboard stats
        stats = self.rm.get_dashboard_stats(pid, sid)
        self.assertIn('total_expected', stats)
        self.assertGreater(stats['total_expected'], 0)

        # 9. Add shortage
        shortage_ids = self.shm.add_shortage(pid, "ظˆط§ط­ط¯ ط§ط¬ط±ط§غŒغŒ", "ط¨ط®ط´ ط§ظ†طھط¸ط§ظ…ط§طھ", 1, "ط¢ظ‚ط§", "ع©ظ…ط¨ظˆط¯ ظ†غŒط±ظˆ")
        self.assertEqual(len(shortage_ids), 1)

        # 10. Archive project
        self.proj_mgr.archive_project(pid)
        proj = self.proj_mgr.get_project(pid)
        self.assertEqual(proj['status'], 'ARCHIVED')

    def test_class_project_schedule_flow(self):
        """Test class project: schedule -> sessions -> attendance"""
        pid = self._insert_project("ع©ظ„ط§ط³ ط­ع©ظ…طھ", "ع©ظ„ط§ط³")
        
        # Create schedule
        sched_id = self.am.create_schedule(pid, "ع©ظ„ط§ط³ ط­ع©ظ…طھ", "ط³ظ‡â€Œط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "16:00")
        
        # Add staff to schedule
        staff_id = self.sm.add_staff_member(pid, "ط§ط³طھط§ط¯ ط§ط­ظ…ط¯غŒ", gender="ط¢ظ‚ط§", schedule_id=sched_id)
        
        # Create session linked to schedule
        sid = self.am.create_session(pid, "ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ غ±", session_date="2026-09-30", schedule_id=sched_id)
        
        # Verify attendance from schedule_staff
        records = self.am.get_session_attendance(pid, sid)
        names = [r['name'] for r in records]
        self.assertIn("ط§ط³طھط§ط¯ ط§ط­ظ…ط¯غŒ", names)

        # Verify no fallback to project_staff for other sessions without schedule
        # (all class sessions must have schedule)
        sess = self.am.get_session(sid)
        self.assertEqual(sess['schedule_id'], sched_id)


class TestConfigPaths(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_fix1_no_hardcoded_tmp(self):
        """FIX 1: DEFAULT_SQLITE_PATH should not default to /tmp"""
        import config
        # Clear the env var to test the default
        original = os.environ.pop('SQLITE_PATH', None)
        try:
            # Reload to get the default
            import importlib
            importlib.reload(config)
            default_path = config.DEFAULT_SQLITE_PATH
            # Should be in the data dir, not /tmp
            normalized = default_path.replace('\\', '/')
            self.assertNotIn('/tmp/', normalized,
                            f"FIX 1: DEFAULT_SQLITE_PATH should not use /tmp, got: {default_path}")
        finally:
            if original:
                os.environ['SQLITE_PATH'] = original
            importlib.reload(config)

    def test_fix2_db_backup_uses_tempdir(self):
        """FIX 2: db_manager.backup_database must use tempfile.gettempdir() not /tmp"""
        import db_manager
        with open(db_manager.__file__, encoding='utf-8') as f:
            src = f.read()
        self.assertNotIn('os.path.join("/tmp"', src,
                        'FIX 2: db_manager.py must not use hardcoded /tmp for backup')


class TestSuperAdminGender(unittest.TestCase):
    """FIX 3: Super admin should be seeded with 'ظ†ط§ظ…ط´ط®طµ' not 'ط®ط§ظ†ظ…'"""

    def test_super_admin_seeded_with_unknown_gender(self):
        """FIX 3: New super admin seed should use ظ†ط§ظ…ط´ط®طµ gender"""
        import db_manager as dm
        with open(dm.__file__, encoding='utf-8') as f:
            src = f.read()
        # The seeding code must use 'ظ†ط§ظ…ط´ط®طµ' not 'ط®ط§ظ†ظ…'
        self.assertIn("'ظ†ط§ظ…ط´ط®طµ'", src, "FIX 3: Super admin seed must use 'ظ†ط§ظ…ط´ط®طµ' gender")
        # Verify 'ط®ط§ظ†ظ…' hardcoded in the super admin seed block is gone
        # Find the super admin seed section and check it doesn't have 'ط®ط§ظ†ظ…'
        seed_start = src.find("Seed default global super admins")
        if seed_start != -1:
            seed_section = src[seed_start:seed_start + 400]
            self.assertNotIn("'ط®ط§ظ†ظ…'", seed_section,
                           "FIX 3: Super admin seed block must not use 'ط®ط§ظ†ظ…'")

    def test_super_admin_existing_name_preserved(self):
        """FIX 3: Existing super admin custom name must be preserved on re-seed"""
        db, db_path = make_test_db()
        try:
            conn = db.get_sqlite_connection()
            # Manually set a custom name
            conn.execute("UPDATE users SET staff_name = 'ط§ط­ظ…ط¯ ط±ط¶ط§غŒغŒ' WHERE user_id = 1129742448")
            conn.commit()
            conn.close()

            # Re-running setup_database should NOT overwrite custom name
            db.setup_database()
            conn = db.get_sqlite_connection()
            row = conn.execute("SELECT staff_name FROM users WHERE user_id = 1129742448").fetchone()
            conn.close()
            if row:
                self.assertEqual(row[0], 'ط§ط­ظ…ط¯ ط±ط¶ط§غŒغŒ',
                               "FIX 3: Existing custom name must not be overwritten on re-seed")
        finally:
            try:
                os.remove(db_path)
            except Exception:
                pass


class TestFix5ClassProjectRecurringDays(unittest.TestCase):
    """FIX 5: Class project sessions should start on the correct weekday"""

    def test_class_sessions_aligned_to_weekday(self):
        """FIX 5: Sessions for a ع©ظ„ط§ط³ project must start on the day matching recurring_days"""
        from utils import get_persian_weekday
        from datetime import datetime, timedelta

        # Simulate the fixed logic
        base_dt = datetime(2026, 9, 21)  # A Monday (ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡)
        recurring_days = "ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡"
        valid_days = ["ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "غŒع©ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "ط³ظ‡â€Œط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "ع†ظ‡ط§ط±ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "ظ¾ظ†ط¬â€Œط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "ط¬ظ…ط¹ظ‡"]

        class_start_dt = base_dt
        if recurring_days and recurring_days.strip() in valid_days:
            for offset in range(7):
                candidate = base_dt + timedelta(days=offset)
                if get_persian_weekday(candidate) == recurring_days.strip():
                    class_start_dt = candidate
                    break

        # class_start_dt should be Saturday (ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡), not Monday
        result_day = get_persian_weekday(class_start_dt)
        self.assertEqual(result_day, "ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡",
                       f"FIX 5: First session should be on ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡, got {result_day} ({class_start_dt})")

    def test_class_sessions_weekly_after_start(self):
        """FIX 5: Subsequent sessions should be 7 days apart"""
        from utils import get_persian_weekday
        from datetime import datetime, timedelta

        base_dt = datetime(2026, 9, 21)  # Monday
        recurring_days = "ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡"
        valid_days = ["ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "غŒع©ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "ط³ظ‡â€Œط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "ع†ظ‡ط§ط±ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "ظ¾ظ†ط¬â€Œط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "ط¬ظ…ط¹ظ‡"]

        class_start_dt = base_dt
        for offset in range(7):
            candidate = base_dt + timedelta(days=offset)
            if get_persian_weekday(candidate) == recurring_days.strip():
                class_start_dt = candidate
                break

        # All 4 sessions should be on ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡
        for i in range(1, 5):
            sess_dt = class_start_dt + timedelta(days=7 * (i - 1))
            self.assertEqual(get_persian_weekday(sess_dt), "ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡",
                           f"Session {i} should be on ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡")

    unittest.main(verbosity=2)

import sys

class TestArchitecturalInvariants(unittest.TestCase):

    def test_01_empty_schedule_roster_zero_fallback(self):
        """P0: When schedule has 0 assigned staff, creating a session produces 0 attendance rows."""
        ts = str(datetime.now().timestamp())
        pid = project_manager.create_project(f"ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ ط¨ط¯ظˆظ† ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط¨ط±ظ†ط§ظ…ظ‡ {ts}", project_type="ع©ظ„ط§ط³")
        
        st1 = staff_manager.add_staff_member(pid, "ظ†غŒط±ظˆغŒ ط¹ظ…ظˆظ…غŒ غ±", "09121111111", "ط¢ظ…ظˆط²ط´", "ظ¾ط°غŒط±ط´", gender="ط®ط§ظ†ظ…")
        sched_empty = attendance_manager.create_schedule(pid, "ع©ظ„ط§ط³ ط¨ط¯ظˆظ† ع©ط§ط¯ط±", "ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "16:00")

        sess_id = attendance_manager.create_session(pid, "ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ ط§ظˆظ„ ط®ط§ظ„غŒ", schedule_id=sched_empty)
        att = attendance_manager.get_session_attendance(pid, sess_id)

        self.assertEqual(len(att), 0, "Session of empty schedule must have 0 attendance rows and NEVER fallback to project_staff!")

        admin_uid = 991100
        permission_manager.upsert_user(admin_uid, "ظ…ط¯غŒط± ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡", "ط®ط§ظ†ظ…")
        permission_manager.set_project_user(pid, admin_uid, role="admin")
        ok, msg = attendance_manager.seed_session_roster_explicit(pid, sess_id, actor_user_id=admin_uid)
        att_after_seed = attendance_manager.get_session_attendance(pid, sess_id)
        self.assertEqual(len(att_after_seed), 0, "Explicit seed on empty schedule must NEVER fallback to project_staff!")

    def test_02_schedule_staff_segregation_and_lifecycle(self):
        """P0: Staff in Schedule Monday does not appear in Schedule Sunday session."""
        ts = str(datetime.now().timestamp())
        pid = project_manager.create_project(f"ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ طھظپع©غŒع© ع©ط§ط¯ط± ع©ظ„ط§ط³â€Œظ‡ط§ {ts}", project_type="ع©ظ„ط§ط³")
        sched_mon = attendance_manager.create_schedule(pid, "ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡ ط³ط·ط­ غ±", "ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "16:00")
        sched_sun = attendance_manager.create_schedule(pid, "غŒع©ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡ ط³ط·ط­ غ³", "غŒع©ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "17:00")

        st_mon = staff_manager.add_staff_member(pid, "ط³ط§ط±ط§ ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡â€Œطھط¨ط§ط±", "09121111111", "ط¢ظ…ظˆط²ط´", "ظ¾ط°غŒط±ط´", gender="ط®ط§ظ†ظ…", schedule_id=sched_mon)
        st_sun = staff_manager.add_staff_member(pid, "ظ…ط±غŒظ… غŒع©ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡â€Œطھط¨ط§ط±", "09122222222", "ط¢ظ…ظˆط²ط´", "ظ¾ط°غŒط±ط´", gender="ط®ط§ظ†ظ…", schedule_id=sched_sun)
        st_both = staff_manager.add_staff_member(pid, "ط²ظ‡ط±ط§ ظ‡ط±ط¯ظˆط±ظˆط²", "09123333333", "ط±ط³ط§ظ†ظ‡", "ظ¾ط®ط´", gender="ط®ط§ظ†ظ…", schedule_id=sched_mon)
        staff_manager.assign_staff_to_schedule(sched_sun, st_both)

        sess_mon = attendance_manager.create_session(pid, "ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡ غ±", schedule_id=sched_mon)
        att_mon = attendance_manager.get_session_attendance(pid, sess_mon)
        staff_ids_mon = [s['staff_id'] for s in att_mon]

        self.assertIn(st_mon, staff_ids_mon)
        self.assertIn(st_both, staff_ids_mon)
        self.assertNotIn(st_sun, staff_ids_mon)

        sess_sun = attendance_manager.create_session(pid, "ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ غŒع©ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡ غ±", schedule_id=sched_sun)
        att_sun = attendance_manager.get_session_attendance(pid, sess_sun)
        staff_ids_sun = [s['staff_id'] for s in att_sun]

        self.assertIn(st_sun, staff_ids_sun)
        self.assertIn(st_both, staff_ids_sun)
        self.assertNotIn(st_mon, staff_ids_sun)

    def test_03_authorization_rejects_staff_not_in_session_roster(self):
        """P0: authorize_attendance_action verifies staff is enrolled in session roster."""
        ts = str(datetime.now().timestamp())
        pid = project_manager.create_project(f"ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ ع©ظ†طھط±ظ„ ط¯ط³طھط±ط³غŒ ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ {ts}", project_type="ع©ظ„ط§ط³")
        sched_mon = attendance_manager.create_schedule(pid, "ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "16:00")
        sched_tue = attendance_manager.create_schedule(pid, "ط³ظ‡â€Œط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "ط³ظ‡â€Œط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "16:00")

        st_mon = staff_manager.add_staff_member(pid, "ط¹ظ„غŒ ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "09124444444", "ط¢ظ…ظˆط²ط´", "ظ¾ط°غŒط±ط´", gender="ط¢ظ‚ط§", schedule_id=sched_mon)
        st_tue = staff_manager.add_staff_member(pid, "ط±ط¶ط§ ط³ظ‡â€Œط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "09125555555", "ط¢ظ…ظˆط²ط´", "ظ¾ط°غŒط±ط´", gender="ط¢ظ‚ط§", schedule_id=sched_tue)

        sess_mon = attendance_manager.create_session(pid, "ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡ غ±", schedule_id=sched_mon)

        op_uid = 992200
        permission_manager.upsert_user(op_uid, "ط§ظ¾ط±ط§طھظˆط± ط¨ط±ط§ط¯ط±ط§ظ†", "ط¢ظ‚ط§")
        permission_manager.set_project_user(pid, op_uid, role="operator", gender="ط¢ظ‚ط§")

        allowed, _, _ = permission_manager.authorize_attendance_action(op_uid, pid, sess_mon, st_mon, "update_attendance")
        self.assertTrue(allowed)

        allowed, reason, _ = permission_manager.authorize_attendance_action(op_uid, pid, sess_mon, st_tue, "update_attendance")
        self.assertFalse(allowed)

    def test_04_strict_session_bounded_phone_sync(self):
        """P0: sync_same_phone strictly updates attendance of current session only."""
        ts = str(datetime.now().timestamp())
        pid = project_manager.create_project(f"ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ طھط³طھ ط³غŒظ†ع© طھظ„ظپظ† {ts}", project_type="ع©ظ„ط§ط³")
        sched_mon = attendance_manager.create_schedule(pid, "ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "16:00")
        sched_sun = attendance_manager.create_schedule(pid, "غŒع©ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "غŒع©ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "17:00")

        shared_phone = "09129990011"
        st_ali = staff_manager.add_staff_member(pid, "ط¹ظ„غŒ ط§ط­ظ…ط¯غŒ", shared_phone, "ط¢ظ…ظˆط²ط´", "ظ¾ط°غŒط±ط´", gender="ط¢ظ‚ط§", schedule_id=sched_mon)
        st_reza = staff_manager.add_staff_member(pid, "ط±ط¶ط§ ط§ط­ظ…ط¯غŒ", shared_phone, "ط®ط¯ظ…ط§طھ", "ظ¾ط°غŒط±ط§غŒغŒ", gender="ط¢ظ‚ط§", schedule_id=sched_sun)

        sess_mon = attendance_manager.create_session(pid, "ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡ غ±", schedule_id=sched_mon)
        sess_sun = attendance_manager.create_session(pid, "ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ غŒع©ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡ غ±", schedule_id=sched_sun)

        attendance_manager.update_attendance_status(pid, sess_mon, st_ali, "ط­ط§ط¶ط±", sync_same_phone=True)

        rec_ali_mon = attendance_manager.get_staff_session_attendance(pid, sess_mon, st_ali)
        self.assertEqual(rec_ali_mon['status'], "ط­ط§ط¶ط±")

        rec_reza_mon = attendance_manager.get_staff_session_attendance(pid, sess_mon, st_reza)
        self.assertIsNone(rec_reza_mon)

        rec_reza_sun = attendance_manager.get_staff_session_attendance(pid, sess_sun, st_reza)
        self.assertEqual(rec_reza_sun['status'], "")

    def test_05_schedule_project_ownership_enforcement(self):
        """P1: Cannot assign cross-project schedule."""
        ts = str(datetime.now().timestamp())
        pid1 = project_manager.create_project(f"ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ ط§طµظ„غŒ {ts}")
        pid2 = project_manager.create_project(f"ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ ط«ط§ظ†ظˆغŒظ‡ {ts}")

        sched1 = attendance_manager.create_schedule(pid1, "ط¨ط±ظ†ط§ظ…ظ‡ ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ غ±", "ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "10:00")
        st2 = staff_manager.add_staff_member(pid2, "ع©ط§ط¯ط± ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ غ²", "09127778899", "ط¨ط®ط´", "ظˆط§ط­ط¯", gender="ط¢ظ‚ط§")

        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as ctx:
            attendance_manager.create_session(pid2, "ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ ظ†ط§ظ…ط¹طھط¨ط±", schedule_id=sched1)
        self.assertIn("ظ…طھط¹ظ„ظ‚ ط¨ظ‡ ط§غŒظ† ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ ظ†غŒط³طھ", str(ctx.exception))

        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as ctx:
            staff_manager.assign_staff_to_schedule(sched1, st2)
        self.assertIn("غŒع©ط³ط§ظ† ظ†غŒط³طھظ†ط¯", str(ctx.exception))

    def test_06_no_tmp_sqlite_fallback_on_unwritable_path(self):
        """P1: Unwritable path raises DatabaseUnavailableError and creates NO file in /tmp."""
        temp_parent = tempfile.mkdtemp()
        os.chmod(temp_parent, 0o555)
        unwritable_db = os.path.join(temp_parent, "test_locked_dir", "test_fail.db")
        expected_tmp_fallback = "/tmp/test_fail.db"

        if os.path.exists(expected_tmp_fallback):
            os.remove(expected_tmp_fallback)

        try:
            with self.assertRaises(DatabaseUnavailableError):
                DatabaseManager(db_path=unwritable_db)

            self.assertFalse(os.path.exists(expected_tmp_fallback), "Hidden /tmp database fallback must NOT be created!")
        finally:
            os.chmod(temp_parent, 0o777)
            shutil.rmtree(temp_parent)

    def test_07_create_session_atomicity(self):
        """P1: create_session must be atomic."""
        ts = str(datetime.now().timestamp())
        pid = project_manager.create_project(f"ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ ط§طھظ…غŒع© {ts}")
        st = staff_manager.add_staff_member(pid, "ظ†غŒط±ظˆغŒ طھط³طھ ط§طھظ…غŒع©", "09120000000", "ط¢ظ…ظˆط²ط´", "ع©ظ„ط§ط³", gender="ط®ط§ظ†ظ…")

        sess_id = attendance_manager.create_session(pid, "ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ ط§طھظ…غŒع© غ±")
        sess = attendance_manager.get_session(sess_id)
        att = attendance_manager.get_session_attendance(pid, sess_id)

        self.assertIsNotNone(sess)
        self.assertEqual(len(att), 1)
        self.assertEqual(att[0]['staff_id'], st)

    def test_08_backup_integrity_and_operational_restore(self):
        """P2: Backup integrity check and real operational query test on restored DB."""
        backup_path = db_instance.backup_database(label="operational_test")
        self.assertIsNotNone(backup_path)
        self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(backup_path))
        self.assertTrue(db_instance.verify_backup_integrity(backup_path))

        test_restore_path = "/tmp/test_restore_operational.db"
        if os.path.exists(test_restore_path):
            os.remove(test_restore_path)

        self.assertTrue(db_instance.restore_backup(backup_path, destination_path=test_restore_path))

        restored_conn = sqlite3.connect(test_restore_path)
        cur = restored_conn.cursor()
        cur.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM projects")
        p_cnt = cur.fetchone()[0]
        cur.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM users")
        u_cnt = cur.fetchone()[0]
        restored_conn.close()

        self.assertGreaterEqual(p_cnt, 1)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(u_cnt, 1)

        if os.path.exists(test_restore_path):
            os.remove(test_restore_path)

    def test_09_excel_schedule_roster_lifecycle(self):
        """P0: Excel sheet manages its schedule roster lifecycle."""
        ts = str(datetime.now().timestamp())
        pid = project_manager.create_project(f"ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ طھط³طھ ع†ط±ط®ظ‡ ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط§ع©ط³ظ„ {ts}", project_type="ع©ظ„ط§ط³")
        sched_mon = attendance_manager.create_schedule(pid, "ع©ظ„ط§ط³ ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "16:00")

        sess_1 = attendance_manager.create_session(pid, "ع©ظ„ط§ط³ ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", session_date="2026-10-01", schedule_id=sched_mon)

        wb1 = openpyxl.Workbook()
        ws1 = wb1.active
        ws1.title = "ع©ظ„ط§ط³ ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡"
        headers = ["ط±ط¯غŒظپ", "ظˆط§ط­ط¯", "ط¨ط®ط´", "ظ†ط§ظ… ظˆ ظ†ط§ظ… ط®ط§ظ†ظˆط§ط¯ع¯غŒ", "ط³ظ…طھ", "ط¹ظ†ظˆط§ظ† ع©ط§ط±طھ", "ط´ظ…ط§ط±ظ‡ طھظ…ط§ط³", "ط³ط§ط¹طھ ط­ط¶ظˆط±", "ظ¾غŒع¯غŒط±غŒ طھط§ط®غŒط±", "طھظˆط¶غŒط­ط§طھ", "ظˆط¶ط¹غŒطھ ع©ط§ط±طھ", "ظˆط¶ط¹غŒطھ ط­ط¶ظˆط±", "ط¬ظ†ط³غŒطھ", "ظپط¹ط§ظ„ ط¯ط± ع†ظ†ط¯ ط¨ط®ط´طں"]
        ws1.append(headers)
        ws1.append([1, "ط¢ظ…ظˆط²ط´", "ظ¾ط°غŒط±ط´", "ط¹ظ„غŒ ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط§ظˆظ„", "ظ†غŒط±ظˆ", "ظ¾ط°غŒط±ط´", "09121110001", "16:00", "", "", "طھط­ظˆغŒظ„ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡ ط´ط¯", "ط­ط§ط¶ط±", "ط¢ظ‚ط§", "ط®غŒط±"])
        path1 = f"/tmp/test_roster_v1_{ts}.xlsx"
        wb1.save(path1)
        wb1.close()

        excel_manager.import_project_excel(pid, path1)

        sched_staff = staff_manager.list_schedule_staff(sched_mon)
        self.assertEqual(len(sched_staff), 1)
        self.assertEqual(sched_staff[0]['name'], "ط¹ظ„غŒ ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط§ظˆظ„")

        sess_2 = attendance_manager.create_session(pid, "ع©ظ„ط§ط³ ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡ غ²", session_date="2026-10-08", schedule_id=sched_mon)
        wb2 = openpyxl.Workbook()
        ws2 = wb2.active
        ws2.title = "ع©ظ„ط§ط³ ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡ غ²"
        ws2.append(headers)
        ws2.append([1, "ط¢ظ…ظˆط²ط´", "ظ¾ط°غŒط±ط´", "ط³ط§ط±ط§ ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط¬ط¯غŒط¯", "ظ†غŒط±ظˆ", "ظ¾ط°غŒط±ط´", "09121110002", "16:00", "", "", "طھط­ظˆغŒظ„ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡ ط´ط¯", "ط­ط§ط¶ط±", "ط®ط§ظ†ظ…", "ط®غŒط±"])
        path2 = f"/tmp/test_roster_v2_{ts}.xlsx"
        wb2.save(path2)
        wb2.close()

        excel_manager.import_project_excel(pid, path2)

        active_sched_staff = staff_manager.list_schedule_staff(sched_mon, active_only=True)
        active_names = [s['name'] for s in active_sched_staff]
        self.assertIn("ط³ط§ط±ط§ ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط¬ط¯غŒط¯", active_names)
        self.assertNotIn("ط¹ظ„غŒ ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط§ظˆظ„", active_names)

        all_proj_staff = staff_manager.list_staff(pid, active_only=False)
        all_names = [s['name'] for s in all_proj_staff]
        self.assertIn("ط¹ظ„غŒ ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط§ظˆظ„", all_names)

        att_s1 = attendance_manager.get_session_attendance(pid, sess_1)
        self.assertEqual(len(att_s1), 1)
        self.assertEqual(att_s1[0]['name'], "ط¹ظ„غŒ ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط§ظˆظ„")

        for p in [path1, path2]:
            if os.path.exists(p): os.remove(p)

    def test_10_atomic_add_staff_member_with_schedule(self):
        """P1: add_staff_member with schedule_id is atomic."""
        ts = str(datetime.now().timestamp())
        pid1 = project_manager.create_project(f"ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ ط§طھظ…غŒع© غŒع© {ts}")
        pid2 = project_manager.create_project(f"ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ ط§طھظ…غŒع© ط¯ظˆ {ts}")

        sched_other = attendance_manager.create_schedule(pid2, "ط¨ط±ظ†ط§ظ…ظ‡ ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ غ²", "ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "10:00")

        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            staff_manager.add_staff_member(pid1, "ظ†غŒط±ظˆغŒ ظ†ط§ظ…ط¹طھط¨ط±", "09128889900", "ظˆط§ط­ط¯", "ط¨ط®ط´", schedule_id=sched_other)

        st_list = staff_manager.list_staff(pid1)
        self.assertEqual(len(st_list), 0)

    def test_11_excel_import_two_phase_atomic_rollback(self):
        """P0: If DB error occurs during import, physical project_data.xlsx is untouched."""
        ts = str(datetime.now().timestamp())
        pid = project_manager.create_project(f"ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ طھط³طھ ط§طھظ…غŒع© ط§ع©ط³ظ„ {ts}")
        original_excel_path = excel_manager.get_project_excel_path(pid)

        wb_orig = openpyxl.Workbook()
        ws_orig = wb_orig.active
        ws_orig.title = "ط´غŒطھ ط§طµظ„غŒ"
        ws_orig.append(["ظ†ط´ط§ظ†ع¯ط± ط§طµظ„غŒ ظ‚ط¨ظ„ ط§ط² ط´ع©ط³طھ"])
        wb_orig.save(original_excel_path)
        wb_orig.close()

        wb_bad = openpyxl.Workbook()
        ws_bad = wb_bad.active
        ws_bad.title = "ط±ظˆط² غ±"
        headers = ["ط±ط¯غŒظپ", "ظˆط§ط­ط¯", "ط¨ط®ط´", "ظ†ط§ظ… ظˆ ظ†ط§ظ… ط®ط§ظ†ظˆط§ط¯ع¯غŒ", "ط³ظ…طھ", "ط¹ظ†ظˆط§ظ† ع©ط§ط±طھ", "ط´ظ…ط§ط±ظ‡ طھظ…ط§ط³", "ط³ط§ط¹طھ ط­ط¶ظˆط±", "ظ¾غŒع¯غŒط±غŒ طھط§ط®غŒط±", "طھظˆط¶غŒط­ط§طھ", "ظˆط¶ط¹غŒطھ ع©ط§ط±طھ", "ظˆط¶ط¹غŒطھ ط­ط¶ظˆط±", "ط¬ظ†ط³غŒطھ", "ظپط¹ط§ظ„ ط¯ط± ع†ظ†ط¯ ط¨ط®ط´طں"]
        ws_bad.append(headers)
        ws_bad.append([1, "ظˆط§ط­ط¯", "ط¨ط®ط´", "ظ†ط§ظ… ظ†غŒط±ظˆ", "ط³ظ…طھ", "ع©ط§ط±طھ", "09120000000", "16:00", "", "", "", "", "ط¢ظ‚ط§", "ط®غŒط±"])
        bad_path = f"/tmp/bad_excel_{ts}.xlsx"
        wb_bad.save(bad_path)
        wb_bad.close()

        real_get_conn = db_instance.get_sqlite_connection
        def failing_conn():
            c = real_get_conn()
            c.execute("CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS fail_trg BEFORE INSERT ON project_staff BEGIN SELECT RAISE(FAIL, 'MOCK_ERROR'); END;")
            return c

        db_instance.get_sqlite_connection = failing_conn
        try:
            with self.assertRaises(Exception):
                excel_manager.import_project_excel(pid, bad_path)

            wb_check = openpyxl.load_workbook(original_excel_path)
            self.assertIn("ط´غŒطھ ط§طµظ„غŒ", wb_check.sheetnames)
            wb_check.close()
        finally:
            db_instance.get_sqlite_connection = real_get_conn
            c_clean = db_instance.get_sqlite_connection()
            c_clean.execute("DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS fail_trg")
            c_clean.close()
            if os.path.exists(bad_path): os.remove(bad_path)

    def test_12_worker_skips_inactive_schedule_and_archived_project(self):
        """P1: Worker skips inactive schedules and archived projects."""
        ts = str(datetime.now().timestamp())
        pid = project_manager.create_project(f"ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ طھط³طھ ظˆط±ع©ط± ط§ط³ع©ط¬ظˆظ„ ط؛غŒط±ظپط¹ط§ظ„ {ts}")
        sched = attendance_manager.create_schedule(pid, "ع©ظ„ط§ط³ ط؛غŒط±ظپط¹ط§ظ„", "ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "16:00")
        today_str = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")

        sess_id = attendance_manager.create_session(pid, "ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ ط§ظ…ط±ظˆط²", session_date=today_str, schedule_id=sched)

        sessions_active = worker_manager.get_today_sessions(pid)
        self.assertEqual(len(sessions_active), 1)

        # Deactivate schedule -> skipped
        attendance_manager.set_schedule_active(sched, is_active=False)
        sessions_inactive = worker_manager.get_today_sessions(pid)
        self.assertEqual(sessions_inactive, [])

        # Reactivate schedule, archive project -> skipped
        attendance_manager.set_schedule_active(sched, is_active=True)
        project_manager.archive_project(pid)
        sessions_archived = worker_manager.get_today_sessions(pid)
        self.assertEqual(sessions_archived, [])

    def test_13_pure_historical_snapshot_no_fallback(self):
        """P1: Historical attendance reads exclusively from snapshot and never leaks future changes."""
        ts = str(datetime.now().timestamp())
        pid = project_manager.create_project(f"ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ طھط³طھ ط³ظ„ط§ظ…طھ ط§ط³ظ†ظ¾ ط´ط§طھ {ts}")
        st_id = staff_manager.add_staff_member(pid, "ظ†ط§ظ… طھط§ط±غŒط®غŒ ط§ظˆظ„غŒظ‡", "09121112233", "ظˆط§ط­ط¯ ظ‚ط¯غŒظ…", "ط¨ط®ط´ ظ‚ط¯غŒظ…", position="ظ…ط³ط¦ظˆظ„", gender="ط®ط§ظ†ظ…")
        sess_id = attendance_manager.create_session(pid, "ط±ظˆط² ط§ظˆظ„ طھط§ط±غŒط®غŒ")

        att1 = attendance_manager.get_session_attendance(pid, sess_id)
        self.assertEqual(att1[0]['name'], "ظ†ط§ظ… طھط§ط±غŒط®غŒ ط§ظˆظ„غŒظ‡")
        self.assertEqual(att1[0]['unit'], "ظˆط§ط­ط¯ ظ‚ط¯غŒظ…")

        staff_manager.update_staff_field(st_id, "name", "ظ†ط§ظ… طھط؛غŒغŒط±غŒط§ظپطھظ‡ ط¬ط¯غŒط¯")
        staff_manager.update_staff_field(st_id, "unit", "ظˆط§ط­ط¯ ع©ط§ظ…ظ„ط§ ظ…طھظپط§ظˆطھ")

        att_frozen = attendance_manager.get_session_attendance(pid, sess_id)
        self.assertEqual(att_frozen[0]['name'], "ظ†ط§ظ… طھط§ط±غŒط®غŒ ط§ظˆظ„غŒظ‡")
        self.assertEqual(att_frozen[0]['unit'], "ظˆط§ط­ط¯ ظ‚ط¯غŒظ…")

    def test_14_schedule_staff_reactivation_on_rejoin(self):
        """P1: Rejoining a schedule clears end_session_id and reactivates membership."""
        ts = str(datetime.now().timestamp())
        pid = project_manager.create_project(f"ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ طھط³طھ ظˆط±ظˆط¯ ظ…ط¬ط¯ط¯ {ts}")
        sched = attendance_manager.create_schedule(pid, "ع©ظ„ط§ط³ ط¨ط§ط²ع¯ط´طھ", "ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "10:00")
        st = staff_manager.add_staff_member(pid, "ظ†غŒط±ظˆغŒ ط¨ط§ط²ع¯ط´طھغŒ", "09123334455", "ط¨ط®ط´", "ظˆط§ط­ط¯", gender="ط¢ظ‚ط§", schedule_id=sched)

        staff_manager.remove_staff_from_schedule(sched, st, end_session_id=5)
        active_staff = staff_manager.list_schedule_staff(sched, active_only=True)
        self.assertEqual(len(active_staff), 0)

        staff_manager.assign_staff_to_schedule(sched, st, start_session_id=10)
        active_staff_rejoined = staff_manager.list_schedule_staff(sched, active_only=True)
        self.assertEqual(len(active_staff_rejoined), 1)
        self.assertEqual(active_staff_rejoined[0]['sched_start_session'], 10)
        self.assertIsNone(active_staff_rejoined[0]['sched_end_session'])

    def test_15_update_session_field_forbidden_when_attendance_exists(self):
        """
        P0 (New):
        Changing schedule_id for a session that already has attendance records
        is STRICTLY FORBIDDEN and must raise ValueError.
        """
        ts = str(datetime.now().timestamp())
        pid = project_manager.create_project(f"ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ طھط³طھ ط¹ط¯ظ… طھط؛غŒغŒط± ط¨ط±ظ†ط§ظ…ظ‡ {ts}", project_type="ع©ظ„ط§ط³")
        sched_a = attendance_manager.create_schedule(pid, "ط¨ط±ظ†ط§ظ…ظ‡ ط¢", "ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "16:00")
        sched_b = attendance_manager.create_schedule(pid, "ط¨ط±ظ†ط§ظ…ظ‡ ط¨", "ط³ظ‡â€Œط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "17:00")

        st = staff_manager.add_staff_member(pid, "ظ†غŒط±ظˆغŒ ط¨ط±ظ†ط§ظ…ظ‡ ط¢", "09121113344", "ظˆط§ط­ط¯", "ط¨ط®ط´", gender="ط¢ظ‚ط§", schedule_id=sched_a)
        sess = attendance_manager.create_session(pid, "ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", schedule_id=sched_a)

        # Mark attendance
        attendance_manager.update_attendance_status(pid, sess, st, "ط­ط§ط¶ط±")

        # Attempt to change schedule_id of session to sched_b -> ValueError
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as ctx:
            attendance_manager.update_session_field(sess, "schedule_id", sched_b)
        self.assertIn("ط¯ط§ط±ط§غŒ ط³ط§ط¨ظ‚ظ‡ ط­ط¶ظˆط± ظˆ ط؛غŒط§ط¨ ط§ط³طھ ط؛غŒط±ظ…ط¬ط§ط² ظ…غŒâ€Œط¨ط§ط´ط¯", str(ctx.exception))

    def test_16_update_session_field_prevents_date_collision(self):
        """
        P0 (New):
        Updating session_date or schedule_id must prevent date collisions on (project, schedule, date).
        """
        ts = str(datetime.now().timestamp())
        pid = project_manager.create_project(f"ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ طھط³طھ طھط¯ط§ط®ظ„ طھظ‚ظˆغŒظ… {ts}", project_type="ع©ظ„ط§ط³")
        sched = attendance_manager.create_schedule(pid, "ط¨ط±ظ†ط§ظ…ظ‡ ظ‡ظپطھع¯غŒ", "ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "16:00")

        sess1 = attendance_manager.create_session(pid, "ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ ط§ظˆظ„", session_date="2026-10-01", schedule_id=sched)
        sess2 = attendance_manager.create_session(pid, "ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ ط¯ظˆظ…", session_date="2026-10-08", schedule_id=sched)

        # Attempt to update sess2 date to 2026-10-01 (collides with sess1) -> ValueError
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as ctx:
            attendance_manager.update_session_field(sess2, "session_date", "2026-10-01")
        self.assertIn("ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ ط¯غŒع¯ط±غŒ ط§ط² ظ‚ط¨ظ„ ظˆط¬ظˆط¯ ط¯ط§ط±ط¯", str(ctx.exception))

    def test_17_no_fuzzy_matching_excel_schedule(self):
        """
        P0 (New):
        Excel Import must NOT use fuzzy substring matching between similar schedule names.
        Schedules 'ط³ط·ط­ غ³ ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡' and 'ط³ط·ط­ غ³ ط³ظ‡â€Œط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡' must never be confused.
        """
        ts = str(datetime.now().timestamp())
        pid = project_manager.create_project(f"ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ طھط³طھ طھط·ط¨غŒظ‚ ط¯ظ‚غŒظ‚ {ts}", project_type="ع©ظ„ط§ط³")
        sched_mon = attendance_manager.create_schedule(pid, "ط³ط·ط­ غ³ ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "16:00")
        sched_tue = attendance_manager.create_schedule(pid, "ط³ط·ط­ غ³ ط³ظ‡â€Œط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "ط³ظ‡â€Œط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "16:00")

        st_mon = staff_manager.add_staff_member(pid, "ظ†غŒط±ظˆغŒ ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "09121110001", "ظˆط§ط­ط¯", "ط¨ط®ط´", gender="ط¢ظ‚ط§", schedule_id=sched_mon)
        st_tue = staff_manager.add_staff_member(pid, "ظ†غŒط±ظˆغŒ ط³ظ‡â€Œط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "09121110002", "ظˆط§ط­ط¯", "ط¨ط®ط´", gender="ط¢ظ‚ط§", schedule_id=sched_tue)

        # Create Excel with exact sheet name "ط³ط·ط­ غ³ ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡"
        wb = openpyxl.Workbook()
        ws = wb.active
        ws.title = "ط³ط·ط­ غ³ ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡"
        headers = ["ط±ط¯غŒظپ", "ظˆط§ط­ط¯", "ط¨ط®ط´", "ظ†ط§ظ… ظˆ ظ†ط§ظ… ط®ط§ظ†ظˆط§ط¯ع¯غŒ", "ط³ظ…طھ", "ط¹ظ†ظˆط§ظ† ع©ط§ط±طھ", "ط´ظ…ط§ط±ظ‡ طھظ…ط§ط³", "ط³ط§ط¹طھ ط­ط¶ظˆط±", "ظ¾غŒع¯غŒط±غŒ طھط§ط®غŒط±", "طھظˆط¶غŒط­ط§طھ", "ظˆط¶ط¹غŒطھ ع©ط§ط±طھ", "ظˆط¶ط¹غŒطھ ط­ط¶ظˆط±", "ط¬ظ†ط³غŒطھ", "ظپط¹ط§ظ„ ط¯ط± ع†ظ†ط¯ ط¨ط®ط´طں"]
        ws.append(headers)
        ws.append([1, "ظˆط§ط­ط¯", "ط¨ط®ط´", "ظ†غŒط±ظˆغŒ ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "ظ†غŒط±ظˆ", "ع©ط§ط±طھ", "09121110001", "16:00", "", "", "", "ط­ط§ط¶ط±", "ط¢ظ‚ط§", "ط®غŒط±"])
        path = f"/tmp/test_exact_sched_{ts}.xlsx"
        wb.save(path)
        wb.close()

        try:
            excel_manager.import_project_excel(pid, path)

            # Session created must be strictly tied to sched_mon, NOT sched_tue!
            sess = attendance_manager.get_session_by_name(pid, "ط³ط·ط­ غ³ ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", schedule_id=sched_mon)
            self.assertIsNotNone(sess)
            self.assertEqual(sess['schedule_id'], sched_mon)

            # Sched Tue roster must be completely untouched
            tue_staff = staff_manager.list_schedule_staff(sched_tue)
            self.assertEqual(len(tue_staff), 1)
            self.assertEqual(tue_staff[0]['name'], "ظ†غŒط±ظˆغŒ ط³ظ‡â€Œط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡")
        finally:
            if os.path.exists(path): os.remove(path)

    def test_18_excel_sheet_removal_only_deactivates_that_schedule(self):
        """
        P0 (New):
        Removing a staff member from Sheet Monday ONLY deactivates their membership in Schedule Monday.
        Their membership in Schedule Tuesday and their record in project_staff remain 100% active.
        """
        ts = str(datetime.now().timestamp())
        pid = project_manager.create_project(f"ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ طھط³طھ ط­ط°ظپ ط§ظ†طھط®ط§ط¨غŒ {ts}", project_type="ع©ظ„ط§ط³")
        sched_mon = attendance_manager.create_schedule(pid, "ع©ظ„ط§ط³ ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "16:00")
        sched_tue = attendance_manager.create_schedule(pid, "ع©ظ„ط§ط³ ط³ظ‡â€Œط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "ط³ظ‡â€Œط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "16:00")

        # Staff Ali is member of BOTH Monday and Tuesday schedules
        st_ali = staff_manager.add_staff_member(pid, "ط¹ظ„غŒ ط¯ظˆع©ظ„ط§ط³ظ‡", "09121118899", "ظˆط§ط­ط¯", "ط¨ط®ط´", gender="ط¢ظ‚ط§", schedule_id=sched_mon)
        staff_manager.assign_staff_to_schedule(sched_tue, st_ali)

        # Other staff in Monday
        st_other = staff_manager.add_staff_member(pid, "ظ‡ظ…ع©ط§ط± ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "09121119900", "ظˆط§ط­ط¯", "ط¨ط®ط´", gender="ط¢ظ‚ط§", schedule_id=sched_mon)

        # Verify initial active membership in both
        self.assertEqual(len(staff_manager.list_schedule_staff(sched_mon, active_only=True)), 2)
        self.assertEqual(len(staff_manager.list_schedule_staff(sched_tue, active_only=True)), 1)

        # Import Sheet Monday containing ONLY 'ظ‡ظ…ع©ط§ط± ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡' (Ali is omitted / removed)
        wb = openpyxl.Workbook()
        ws = wb.active
        ws.title = "ع©ظ„ط§ط³ ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡"
        headers = ["ط±ط¯غŒظپ", "ظˆط§ط­ط¯", "ط¨ط®ط´", "ظ†ط§ظ… ظˆ ظ†ط§ظ… ط®ط§ظ†ظˆط§ط¯ع¯غŒ", "ط³ظ…طھ", "ط¹ظ†ظˆط§ظ† ع©ط§ط±طھ", "ط´ظ…ط§ط±ظ‡ طھظ…ط§ط³", "ط³ط§ط¹طھ ط­ط¶ظˆط±", "ظ¾غŒع¯غŒط±غŒ طھط§ط®غŒط±", "طھظˆط¶غŒط­ط§طھ", "ظˆط¶ط¹غŒطھ ع©ط§ط±طھ", "ظˆط¶ط¹غŒطھ ط­ط¶ظˆط±", "ط¬ظ†ط³غŒطھ", "ظپط¹ط§ظ„ ط¯ط± ع†ظ†ط¯ ط¨ط®ط´طں"]
        ws.append(headers)
        ws.append([1, "ظˆط§ط­ط¯", "ط¨ط®ط´", "ظ‡ظ…ع©ط§ط± ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "ظ†غŒط±ظˆ", "ع©ط§ط±طھ", "09121119900", "16:00", "", "", "", "ط­ط§ط¶ط±", "ط¢ظ‚ط§", "ط®غŒط±"])
        path = f"/tmp/test_omit_ali_{ts}.xlsx"
        wb.save(path)
        wb.close()

        try:
            excel_manager.import_project_excel(pid, path)

            # 1. Ali MUST be deactivated in Schedule Monday
            mon_active = staff_manager.list_schedule_staff(sched_mon, active_only=True)
            mon_names = [s['name'] for s in mon_active]
            self.assertNotIn("ط¹ظ„غŒ ط¯ظˆع©ظ„ط§ط³ظ‡", mon_names, "Ali must be inactive in Schedule Monday!")

            # 2. Ali MUST remain ACTIVE in Schedule Tuesday!
            tue_active = staff_manager.list_schedule_staff(sched_tue, active_only=True)
            tue_names = [s['name'] for s in tue_active]
            self.assertIn("ط¹ظ„غŒ ط¯ظˆع©ظ„ط§ط³ظ‡", tue_names, "Ali must remain ACTIVE in Schedule Tuesday!")

            # 3. Ali MUST remain in project_staff as active!
            proj_ali = staff_manager.get_staff_member(st_ali)
            self.assertEqual(proj_ali['is_active'], 1, "Ali must remain active in project_staff!")
        finally:
            if os.path.exists(path): os.remove(path)


    def test_19_historical_attendance_pure_snapshot_all_fields(self):
        """P0: get_session_attendance reads all historical fields purely from attendance table with zero fallback."""
        ts = int(time.time() * 1000)
        pid = project_manager.create_project(f"Proj Hist Snapshot {ts}")
        sched_id = attendance_manager.create_schedule(pid, "ع©ظ„ط§ط³ ط±ظˆط²ط§ظ†ظ‡", "ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "16:00")

        staff_id = staff_manager.add_staff_member(
            pid, "ظ…ط­ظ…ط¯ ط±ط¶ط§غŒغŒ", phone="09121112233", unit="ط±ط³ط§ظ†ظ‡", section="ط¹ع©ط§ط³غŒ",
            position="ط³ط±ظ¾ط±ط³طھ", card_title="ع©ط§ط±طھ ط¹ع©ط§ط³غŒ", shift_time="08:00",
            gender="ط¢ظ‚ط§", is_multi_section="ط¨ظ„ظ‡", staff_code="CODE-001", schedule_id=sched_id
        )

        sess_1 = attendance_manager.create_session(pid, "ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ ط§ظˆظ„", session_date="2026-09-10", schedule_id=sched_id)
        attendance_manager.update_attendance_status(pid, sess_1, staff_id, "ط­ط§ط¶ط±")

        # Now mutate current staff in project_staff: change phone, card_title, shift_time, unit, etc.
        conn = db_instance.get_sqlite_connection()
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        cursor.execute("""
        UPDATE project_staff 
        SET phone = '09359998877', unit = 'ط±ظˆط§ط¨ط· ط¹ظ…ظˆظ…غŒ', card_title = 'ع©ط§ط±طھ ط±ظˆط§ط¨ط· ط¹ظ…ظˆظ…غŒ',
            shift_time = '14:00', name = 'ظ…ط­ظ…ط¯ ط±ط¶ط§غŒغŒ ط¬ط¯غŒط¯'
        WHERE id = ?
        """, (staff_id,))
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()

        # Read historical attendance: MUST return the snapshot from session 1, NOT current values
        hist_att = attendance_manager.get_session_attendance(pid, sess_1)
        self.assertEqual(len(hist_att), 1)
        rec = hist_att[0]
        self.assertEqual(rec['name'], "ظ…ط­ظ…ط¯ ط±ط¶ط§غŒغŒ")
        self.assertEqual(rec['phone'], "09121112233")
        self.assertEqual(rec['unit'], "ط±ط³ط§ظ†ظ‡")
        self.assertEqual(rec['card_title'], "ع©ط§ط±طھ ط¹ع©ط§ط³غŒ")
        self.assertEqual(rec['shift_time'], "08:00")
        self.assertEqual(rec['is_multi_section'], "ط¨ظ„ظ‡")
        self.assertEqual(rec['staff_code'], "CODE-001")

    def test_20_class_project_smart_sessions_creates_exact_n_sessions_with_sequential_dates(self):
        """P0: create_project_with_smart_sessions creates exactly N sessions with sequential weekly dates."""
        ts = int(time.time() * 1000)
        pid, first_sid = project_manager.create_project_with_smart_sessions(
            name=f"ع©ظ„ط§ط³ ط­ع©ظ…طھ ط³ط·ط­ غ± {ts}",
            project_type="ع©ظ„ط§ط³",
            total_sessions=13,
            recurring_days="غŒع©ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡",
            activation_time="16:00",
            start_date="2026-10-04"
        )
        sessions = attendance_manager.list_sessions(pid, include_cancelled=False)
        self.assertEqual(len(sessions), 13, f"Expected 13 sessions, got {len(sessions)}")
        
        # Verify sequential weekly dates
        for idx, s in enumerate(sessions):
            self.assertEqual(s['name'], f"ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ {idx + 1}")
            expected_date = (datetime(2026, 10, 4) + timedelta(days=7 * idx)).strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
            self.assertEqual(s['session_date'], expected_date)

    def test_21_worker_recurring_schedule_multi_week_matching(self):
        """P1: Worker get_today_sessions accurately matches recurring scheduled sessions across dates."""
        ts = int(time.time() * 1000)
        pid = project_manager.create_project(f"Proj Worker Rec {ts}", project_type="ع©ظ„ط§ط³")
        sched_id = attendance_manager.create_schedule(pid, "ع©ظ„ط§ط³ ظ‡ظپطھع¯غŒ", "غŒع©ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "16:00")

        s1 = attendance_manager.create_session(pid, "ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ ط§ظˆظ„", session_date="2026-10-04", day_of_week="غŒع©ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", schedule_id=sched_id)
        s2 = attendance_manager.create_session(pid, "ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ ط¯ظˆظ…", session_date="2026-10-11", day_of_week="غŒع©ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", schedule_id=sched_id)

        # Mock datetime in worker
        from unittest.mock import patch
        with patch('worker_manager.datetime') as mock_dt:
            mock_dt.now.return_value = datetime(2026, 10, 4, 16, 0)
            mock_dt.strftime = datetime.strftime
            res1 = worker_manager.get_today_sessions(pid)
            self.assertEqual(len(res1), 1)
            self.assertEqual(res1[0]['id'], s1)

        with patch('worker_manager.datetime') as mock_dt:
            mock_dt.now.return_value = datetime(2026, 10, 11, 16, 0)
            mock_dt.strftime = datetime.strftime
            res2 = worker_manager.get_today_sessions(pid)
            self.assertEqual(len(res2), 1)
            self.assertEqual(res2[0]['id'], s2)

        # Off day: no session
        with patch('worker_manager.datetime') as mock_dt:
            mock_dt.now.return_value = datetime(2026, 10, 5, 16, 0)
            mock_dt.strftime = datetime.strftime
            res_off = worker_manager.get_today_sessions(pid)
            self.assertEqual(len(res_off), 0)

    def test_22_excel_import_class_sheet_without_schedule_fails_explicitly(self):
        """P0: In a class project, an Excel sheet that does not match an active schedule fails import with clear error."""
        ts = int(time.time() * 1000)
        pid = project_manager.create_project(f"Class Proj Strict {ts}", project_type="ع©ظ„ط§ط³")
        attendance_manager.create_schedule(pid, "ع©ظ„ط§ط³ ط±ط³ظ…غŒ ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "16:00")

        wb = openpyxl.Workbook()
        ws = wb.active
        ws.title = "ع©ظ„ط§ط³ ط·ظ„ط§غŒظ‡ ط­ع©ظ…طھ ط¨ط§ظ†ظˆط§ظ†" # Not matching "ع©ظ„ط§ط³ ط±ط³ظ…غŒ ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡"
        ws.append(["ط±ط¯غŒظپ", "ظ†ط§ظ… ظˆ ظ†ط§ظ… ط®ط§ظ†ظˆط§ط¯ع¯غŒ", "ظˆط§ط­ط¯", "ط¨ط®ط´"])
        ws.append([1, "طھط³طھ ظ†ط§ظ…", "ط¢ظ…ظˆط²ط´", "ط¨ط®ط´ غ±"])
        path = f"/tmp/test_invalid_class_sheet_{ts}.xlsx"
        wb.save(path)
        wb.close()

        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as cm:
            excel_manager.import_project_excel(pid, path)
        self.assertIn("ط¨ظ‡ ظ‡غŒع† ط¨ط±ظ†ط§ظ…ظ‡ (Schedule) ظپط¹ط§ظ„ ط§غŒظ† ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ ظ…طھطµظ„ ظ†غŒط³طھ", str(cm.exception))

    def test_23_excel_backup_retention_strictly_per_project(self):
        """P0: Excel backup retention cleans up strictly per-project, never deleting other projects' backups."""
        ts = int(time.time() * 1000)
        p1 = project_manager.create_project(f"Proj A {ts}")
        p2 = project_manager.create_project(f"Proj B {ts}")

        cat_dir = os.path.join(EXCEL_BACKUPS_DIR, f"test_ret_{ts}")
        os.makedirs(cat_dir, exist_ok=True)

        # Create 10 backups for Project B
        for i in range(10):
            with open(os.path.join(cat_dir, f"project_{p2}_backup_2026-09-16_10-00-{i:02d}.xlsx"), "wb") as f:
                f.write(b"PK000")

        # Create 20 backups for Project A
        for i in range(20):
            with open(os.path.join(cat_dir, f"project_{p1}_backup_2026-09-16_11-00-{i:02d}.xlsx"), "wb") as f:
                f.write(b"PK000")

        # Mock EXCEL_BACKUPS_DIR for testing retention
        from unittest.mock import patch
        with patch('excel_manager.EXCEL_BACKUPS_DIR', os.path.dirname(cat_dir)):
            excel_manager.backup_project_excel(p1, label=f"test_ret_{ts}")

        # Verify Project A now has <= 16 backups
        p1_files = [f for f in os.listdir(cat_dir) if f.startswith(f"project_{p1}_backup_")]
        self.assertLessEqual(len(p1_files), 16)

        # Verify Project B STILL HAS ALL 10 BACKUPS intact!
        p2_files = [f for f in os.listdir(cat_dir) if f.startswith(f"project_{p2}_backup_")]
        self.assertEqual(len(p2_files), 10, "Project B backups were erroneously deleted by Project A cleanup!")

    def test_24_schedule_staff_lifecycle_and_historical_isolation(self):
        """P1: Verifies multi-session lifecycle (Sessions 1-5: A+B+C, Session 6: A+B, Session 7: A+B+D) maintains historical isolation."""
        ts = int(time.time() * 1000)
        pid = project_manager.create_project(f"Proj Lifecycle {ts}", project_type="ع©ظ„ط§ط³")
        sched_id = attendance_manager.create_schedule(pid, "ع©ظ„ط§ط³ طھط®طµطµغŒ", "ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡", "16:00")

        # Staff A, B, C
        s_a = staff_manager.add_staff_member(pid, "ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط§ظ„ظپ", unit="ط¢ظ…ظˆط²ط´", section="ع©ظ„ط§ط³", schedule_id=sched_id)
        s_b = staff_manager.add_staff_member(pid, "ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط¨", unit="ط¢ظ…ظˆط²ط´", section="ع©ظ„ط§ط³", schedule_id=sched_id)
        s_c = staff_manager.add_staff_member(pid, "ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط¬", unit="ط¢ظ…ظˆط²ط´", section="ع©ظ„ط§ط³", schedule_id=sched_id)

        # Sessions 1 to 5
        s1 = attendance_manager.create_session(pid, "ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ غ±", session_date="2026-09-01", schedule_id=sched_id)
        s5 = attendance_manager.create_session(pid, "ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ غµ", session_date="2026-09-29", schedule_id=sched_id)

        self.assertEqual({r['name'] for r in attendance_manager.get_session_attendance(pid, s1)}, {"ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط§ظ„ظپ", "ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط¨", "ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط¬"})
        self.assertEqual({r['name'] for r in attendance_manager.get_session_attendance(pid, s5)}, {"ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط§ظ„ظپ", "ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط¨", "ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط¬"})

        # Session 6: C is removed
        # Simulate Excel update for Session 6 containing only A and B
        wb = openpyxl.Workbook()
        ws = wb.active
        ws.title = "ع©ظ„ط§ط³ طھط®طµطµغŒ"
        headers = ["ط±ط¯غŒظپ", "ظˆط§ط­ط¯", "ط¨ط®ط´", "ظ†ط§ظ… ظˆ ظ†ط§ظ… ط®ط§ظ†ظˆط§ط¯ع¯غŒ", "ط³ظ…طھ", "ط¹ظ†ظˆط§ظ† ع©ط§ط±طھ", "ط´ظ…ط§ط±ظ‡ طھظ…ط§ط³", "ط³ط§ط¹طھ ط­ط¶ظˆط±", "ظ¾غŒع¯غŒط±غŒ طھط§ط®غŒط±", "طھظˆط¶غŒط­ط§طھ", "ظˆط¶ط¹غŒطھ ع©ط§ط±طھ", "ظˆط¶ط¹غŒطھ ط­ط¶ظˆط±", "ط¬ظ†ط³غŒطھ", "ظپط¹ط§ظ„ ط¯ط± ع†ظ†ط¯ ط¨ط®ط´طں"]
        ws.append(headers)
        ws.append([1, "ط¢ظ…ظˆط²ط´", "ع©ظ„ط§ط³", "ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط§ظ„ظپ", "ظ†غŒط±ظˆ", "ع©ظ„ط§ط³", "", "", "", "", "", "ط­ط§ط¶ط±", "ط®ط§ظ†ظ…", "ط®غŒط±"])
        ws.append([2, "ط¢ظ…ظˆط²ط´", "ع©ظ„ط§ط³", "ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط¨", "ظ†غŒط±ظˆ", "ع©ظ„ط§ط³", "", "", "", "", "", "ط­ط§ط¶ط±", "ط®ط§ظ†ظ…", "ط®غŒط±"])
        path = f"/tmp/test_lifecycle_s6_{ts}.xlsx"
        wb.save(path)
        wb.close()
        excel_manager.import_project_excel(pid, path)

        s6 = attendance_manager.create_session(pid, "ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ غ¶", session_date="2026-10-06", schedule_id=sched_id)
        self.assertEqual({r['name'] for r in attendance_manager.get_session_attendance(pid, s6)}, {"ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط§ظ„ظپ", "ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط¨"})

        # Session 7: D is added
        wb7 = openpyxl.Workbook()
        ws7 = wb7.active
        ws7.title = "ع©ظ„ط§ط³ طھط®طµطµغŒ"
        ws7.append(headers)
        ws7.append([1, "ط¢ظ…ظˆط²ط´", "ع©ظ„ط§ط³", "ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط§ظ„ظپ", "ظ†غŒط±ظˆ", "ع©ظ„ط§ط³", "", "", "", "", "", "ط­ط§ط¶ط±", "ط®ط§ظ†ظ…", "ط®غŒط±"])
        ws7.append([2, "ط¢ظ…ظˆط²ط´", "ع©ظ„ط§ط³", "ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط¨", "ظ†غŒط±ظˆ", "ع©ظ„ط§ط³", "", "", "", "", "", "ط­ط§ط¶ط±", "ط®ط§ظ†ظ…", "ط®غŒط±"])
        ws7.append([3, "ط¢ظ…ظˆط²ط´", "ع©ظ„ط§ط³", "ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط¯ط§ظ„", "ظ†غŒط±ظˆ", "ع©ظ„ط§ط³", "", "", "", "", "", "ط­ط§ط¶ط±", "ط®ط§ظ†ظ…", "ط®غŒط±"])
        path7 = f"/tmp/test_lifecycle_s7_{ts}.xlsx"
        wb7.save(path7)
        wb7.close()
        excel_manager.import_project_excel(pid, path7)

        s7 = attendance_manager.create_session(pid, "ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ غ·", session_date="2026-10-13", schedule_id=sched_id)
        self.assertEqual({r['name'] for r in attendance_manager.get_session_attendance(pid, s7)}, {"ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط§ظ„ظپ", "ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط¨", "ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط¯ط§ظ„"})

        # FINAL VERIFICATION: Historical Sessions 1 and 5 MUST STILL BE EXACTLY A + B + C
        self.assertEqual({r['name'] for r in attendance_manager.get_session_attendance(pid, s1)}, {"ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط§ظ„ظپ", "ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط¨", "ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط¬"})
        self.assertEqual({r['name'] for r in attendance_manager.get_session_attendance(pid, s5)}, {"ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط§ظ„ظپ", "ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط¨", "ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط¬"})
        self.assertEqual({r['name'] for r in attendance_manager.get_session_attendance(pid, s6)}, {"ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط§ظ„ظپ", "ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط¨"})
        self.assertEqual({r['name'] for r in attendance_manager.get_session_attendance(pid, s7)}, {"ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط§ظ„ظپ", "ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط¨", "ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط¯ط§ظ„"})

if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

"""

ط§غŒظ† ط§ط³ع©ط±غŒظ¾طھ طھظ…ط§ظ… ظ…ط³غŒط±ظ‡ط§طŒ طھظ…ط§ظ… ط¯ع©ظ…ظ‡â€Œظ‡ط§طŒ طھظ…ط§ظ… ظپط±ط¢غŒظ†ط¯ظ‡ط§غŒ ظˆط±ظˆط¯غŒ ظ¾غŒط§ظ… ظˆ طھظ…ط§ظ…غŒ ظ†ظ‚ط´â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط±غŒ ط±ط§

import sys

import shutil

from datetime import datetime

# ط§ظپط²ظˆط¯ظ† ظ…ط³غŒط± ط¬ط§ط±غŒ ط¨ظ‡ sys.path
_root = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
if _root not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, _root)
