# ظ…ط§ع© ع©ط±ط¯ظ† ط§ط´غŒط§ط، طھظ„ع¯ط±ط§ظ… / ط¨ظ„ظ‡
class DummyChat:
    def __init__(self, chat_id):
        self.id = chat_id

class DummyMessage:
    def __init__(self, chat_id, text="", message_id=1001, document=None):
        self.chat = DummyChat(chat_id)
        self.message_id = message_id
        self.text = text
        self.caption = ""
        self.document = document

class DummyCall:
    def __init__(self, chat_id, data, message_id=1001):
        self.id = f"call_{chat_id}_{data}"
        self.data = data
        self.message = DummyMessage(chat_id, message_id=message_id)

# Mock bot API methods so tests run offline without network delays or 404s
if hasattr(bot, 'bot') and bot.bot:
    bot.bot.send_message = lambda chat_id, text, **kwargs: DummyMessage(chat_id, text)
    bot.bot.edit_message_text = lambda text, chat_id=None, message_id=None, **kwargs: DummyMessage(chat_id, text, message_id)
    bot.bot.edit_message_reply_markup = lambda chat_id=None, message_id=None, **kwargs: True
    bot.bot.answer_callback_query = lambda *args, **kwargs: True
    bot.bot.send_document = lambda *args, **kwargs: True

class BotComprehensiveTestCase(unittest.TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        print("\n" + "="*75)
        print("ًںڑ€ ط§ط¬ط±ط§غŒ ط¢ط²ظ…ظˆظ† ط¬ط§ظ…ط¹ ع©ظ„غŒظ‡ ظ…ط³غŒط±ظ‡ط§طŒ ط¯ع©ظ…ظ‡â€Œظ‡ط§طŒ ظ‡ظ†ط¯ظ„ط±ظ‡ط§ ظˆ ظ†ظ‚ط´â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ط±ط¨ط§طھ")
        print("="*75)
        
        cls.ts = int(datetime.now().timestamp())
        cls.super_admin_id = 90001  # ظ…ط¯غŒط± ط§ط±ط´ط¯ ط³ط±ط§ط³ط±غŒ ظˆ ظ…ط¯غŒط± ط§ط±ط´ط¯ ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡
        cls.admin_id = 90002        # ظ…ط¯غŒط± ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡
        cls.uh_tadarakat = 90003    # ظ…ط³ط¦ظˆظ„ ظˆط§ط­ط¯ طھط¯ط§ط±ع©ط§طھ
        cls.uh_ent = 90004          # ظ…ط³ط¦ظˆظ„ ظˆط§ط­ط¯ ط§ظ†طھط¸ط§ظ…ط§طھ
        cls.operator_id = 90005     # ط§ظ¾ط±ط§طھظˆط±
        cls.viewer_id = 90006       # ط¨غŒظ†ظ†ط¯ظ‡
        cls.stranger_id = 99999     # ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط± ط؛ط±غŒط¨ظ‡ ظˆ ط؛غŒط±ظ…ط¬ط§ط²

        # غ±. ط«ط¨طھ ع©ط§ط±ط¨ط± ط§ط±ط´ط¯ ط³ط±ط§ط³ط±غŒ
        permission_manager.upsert_user(cls.super_admin_id, "ظ…ط¯غŒط± ط§ط±ط´ط¯ طھط³طھ", "ط¢ظ‚ط§", is_global_super_admin=1)
        
        # غ². ط§غŒط¬ط§ط¯ ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ طھط³طھغŒ ط±ظˆغŒط¯ط§ط¯ ط¨ط§ ظ†ط§ظ… غŒع©طھط§
        cls.pid = project_manager.create_project(f"ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ ط±ظˆغŒط¯ط§ط¯ طھط³طھ ط¬ط§ظ…ط¹ {cls.ts}", project_type="ط±ظˆغŒط¯ط§ط¯", description="ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ طھط³طھغŒ")
        cls.s1 = attendance_manager.create_session(cls.pid, "ط±ظˆط² ط¢ظ…ط§ط¯ظ‡ ط³ط§ط²غŒ")
        cls.s2 = attendance_manager.create_session(cls.pid, "ط±ظˆط² 1")
        
        # غ³. ط«ط¨طھ ظˆ طھط®طµغŒطµ ظ†ظ‚ط´â€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡
        permission_manager.upsert_user(cls.admin_id, "ظ…ط¯غŒط± ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ طھط³طھ", "ط®ط§ظ†ظ…")
        permission_manager.set_project_user(cls.pid, cls.admin_id, role="admin", is_active=1)

        permission_manager.upsert_user(cls.uh_tadarakat, "ظ…ط³ط¦ظˆظ„ طھط¯ط§ط±ع©ط§طھ", "ط¢ظ‚ط§")
        permission_manager.set_project_user(cls.pid, cls.uh_tadarakat, role="unit_head", assigned_unit="طھط¯ط§ط±ع©ط§طھ", is_active=1)

        permission_manager.upsert_user(cls.uh_ent, "ظ…ط³ط¦ظˆظ„ ط§ظ†طھط¸ط§ظ…ط§طھ", "ط®ط§ظ†ظ…")
        permission_manager.set_project_user(cls.pid, cls.uh_ent, role="unit_head", assigned_unit="ط§ظ†طھط¸ط§ظ…ط§طھ", is_active=1)

        permission_manager.upsert_user(cls.operator_id, "ط§ظ¾ط±ط§طھظˆط± طھط³طھ", "ط®ط§ظ†ظ…")
        permission_manager.set_project_user(cls.pid, cls.operator_id, role="operator", is_active=1)

        permission_manager.upsert_user(cls.viewer_id, "ط¨غŒظ†ظ†ط¯ظ‡ طھط³طھ", "ط¢ظ‚ط§")
        permission_manager.set_project_user(cls.pid, cls.viewer_id, role="viewer", is_active=1)

        # غ´. ط§غŒط¬ط§ط¯ ظ†غŒط±ظˆظ‡ط§غŒ ع©ط§ط¯ط± ط¯ط± ظˆط§ط­ط¯ظ‡ط§غŒ ظ…ط®طھظ„ظپ
        cls.st1 = staff_manager.add_staff_member(cls.pid, "ط¹ظ„غŒ ط±ط¶ط§غŒغŒ", "طھط¯ط§ط±ع©ط§طھ", "ظ¾ط°غŒط±ط§غŒغŒ", "ط®ط¯ط§ظ…", "ط¢ظ‚ط§", "09121111111")
        cls.st2 = staff_manager.add_staff_member(cls.pid, "ط²ظ‡ط±ط§ ط­ط³غŒظ†غŒ", "ط§ظ†طھط¸ط§ظ…ط§طھ", "ظ…ط³غŒط±", "ط±ط§ظ‡ظ†ظ…ط§", "ط®ط§ظ†ظ…", "09122222222")

        # ط«ط¨طھ ط­ط¶ظˆط± ظˆ ع©ط§ط±طھ ط§ظˆظ„غŒظ‡
        attendance_manager.update_attendance_status(cls.pid, cls.s1, cls.st1, "ط­ط§ط¶ط±")
        attendance_manager.update_card_status(cls.pid, cls.s1, cls.st1, "طھط­ظˆغŒظ„ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡ ط´ط¯")

        # طھظ†ط¸غŒظ… ع©ط§ظ†طھع©ط³طھ ظپط¹ط§ظ„ ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ ظˆ ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡
        for uid in [cls.super_admin_id, cls.admin_id, cls.uh_tadarakat, cls.uh_ent, cls.operator_id, cls.viewer_id]:
            permission_manager.set_user_context(uid, project_id=cls.pid, session_id=cls.s1)

    def test_01_welcome_and_menus(self):
        """طھط³طھ ظ…ظ†ظˆغŒ ط®ظˆط´â€Œط¢ظ…ط¯ع¯ظˆغŒغŒ ظˆ ط³ظˆط¦غŒع† ظ¾ط±ظˆعکظ‡ ط¨ط±ط§غŒ طھظ…ط§ظ… ظ†ظ‚ط´â€Œظ‡ط§"""
        print("\nًں”¹ طھط³طھ غ±: ط§ط±ط³ط§ظ„ /start ظˆ ط¨ط±ط±ط³غŒ ظ…ظ†ظˆغŒ ط¢ط؛ط§ط²غŒظ† ط¨ط±ط§غŒ ظ‡ظ…ظ‡ ظ†ظ‚ط´â€Œظ‡ط§...")
        for uid in [self.super_admin_id, self.admin_id, self.uh_tadarakat, self.operator_id, self.viewer_id, self.stranger_id]:
            bot.send_welcome(DummyMessage(uid, "/start"))
            bot.handle_callbacks(DummyCall(uid, "start_menu"))
            bot.handle_callbacks(DummyCall(uid, "menu_switch_project"))
            bot.handle_callbacks(DummyCall(uid, f"selproj_{self.pid}"))
        print("  âœ… ظ…ظ†ظˆظ‡ط§غŒ ط§ظˆظ„غŒظ‡ ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ظ„ظˆط¯ ط´ط¯ظ†ط¯.")

    def test_02_unit_head_full_flow(self):
        """طھط³طھ ع©ط§ظ…ظ„ ط§ط¹ظ„ط§ظ… ع©ظ…ط¨ظˆط¯ ظ†غŒط±ظˆ طھظˆط³ط· ظ…ط³ط¦ظˆظ„ ظˆط§ط­ط¯ (ط¨ط§ع¯ ع¯ط²ط§ط±ط´ ط´ط¯ظ‡)"""
        print("\nًں”¹ طھط³طھ غ²: ظپط±ط¢غŒظ†ط¯ ع©ط§ظ…ظ„ ط§ط¹ظ„ط§ظ… ع©ظ…ط¨ظˆط¯ ظ†غŒط±ظˆ طھظˆط³ط· ظ…ط³ط¦ظˆظ„ ظˆط§ط­ط¯...")
        uh = self.uh_tadarakat
        
        # غ±. ط¨ط§ط² ع©ط±ط¯ظ† ظ…ظ†ظˆغŒ ط§ط®طھطµط§طµغŒ ظ…ط³ط¦ظˆظ„ ظˆط§ط­ط¯
        bot.handle_callbacks(DummyCall(uh, "menu_unit_head_shortage"))
        
        # غ². ع©ظ„غŒع© ط±ظˆغŒ ط¨ط®ط´ ظ…ظˆط±ط¯ ظ†ط¸ط± (ط¯ظ‚غŒظ‚ط§ظ‹ ظ‡ظ…ط§ظ† ط¯ع©ظ…ظ‡â€Œط§غŒ ع©ظ‡ ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡ ط¨ظˆط¯)
        bot.handle_callbacks(DummyCall(uh, "uh_sec_ظ¾ط°غŒط±ط§غŒغŒ"))
        
        # ط¨ط±ط±ط³غŒ ط«ط¨طھ ظˆط¶ط¹غŒطھ ظ…ظˆظ‚طھ
        st = bot.bot_state.get(uh)
        self.assertIsNotNone(st, "Bot state should be initialized")
        self.assertEqual(st.get('type'), 'uh_shortage')
        self.assertEqual(st.get('section'), 'ظ¾ط°غŒط±ط§غŒغŒ')
        
        # غ³. ظˆط§ط±ط¯ ع©ط±ط¯ظ† طھط¹ط¯ط§ط¯ ظ†غŒط±ظˆغŒ ظ†ط§ظ…ط¹طھط¨ط±
        bot.process_uh_shortage_count(DummyMessage(uh, "ظ…طھظ† ظ†ط§ظ…ط¹طھط¨ط±"))
        self.assertNotIn('count', st)
        
        # غ´. ظˆط§ط±ط¯ ع©ط±ط¯ظ† طھط¹ط¯ط§ط¯ ط¨ط§ ط§ط±ظ‚ط§ظ… ظپط§ط±ط³غŒ
        bot.process_uh_shortage_count(DummyMessage(uh, "غ²"))
        self.assertEqual(st.get('count'), 2)
        
        # غµ. ط§ظ†طھط®ط§ط¨ ط¬ظ†ط³غŒطھ
        bot.handle_callbacks(DummyCall(uh, "uh_grp_ط¢ظ‚ط§"))
        self.assertEqual(st.get('target_group'), "ط¢ظ‚ط§")
        
        # غ¶. ط«ط¨طھ طھظˆط¶غŒط­ط§طھ ع©ظ…ط¨ظˆط¯
        bot.process_uh_shortage_desc(DummyMessage(uh, "ظ†غŒط§ط² ط¨ظ‡ ط¯ظˆ ظ†غŒط±ظˆغŒ ع†ط§غŒâ€Œط±غŒط² ط¨ط±ط§غŒ ط³ط§ظ„ظ† ط¨ط±ط§ط¯ط±ط§ظ†"))
        
        # غ·. طھط³طھ طھط§غŒظ¾ ط¨ط®ط´ ط¨ظ‡ طµظˆط±طھ ط¯ط³طھغŒ
        bot.handle_callbacks(DummyCall(uh, "menu_unit_head_shortage"))
        bot.handle_callbacks(DummyCall(uh, "uh_sec_manual_prompt"))
        bot.process_uh_manual_section(DummyMessage(uh, "ط¨ط®ط´ ط¯ط³طھغŒ ط¬ط¯غŒط¯"))
        self.assertEqual(bot.bot_state[uh]['section'], "ط¨ط®ط´ ط¯ط³طھغŒ ط¬ط¯غŒط¯")
        
        # غ¸. ظ…ط´ط§ظ‡ط¯ظ‡ ع©ط§ط¯ط± ظˆ ظˆط¶ط¹غŒطھ ط­ط¶ظˆط± ظˆط§ط­ط¯ طھظˆط³ط· ظ…ط³ط¦ظˆظ„ ظˆط§ط­ط¯
        bot.handle_callbacks(DummyCall(uh, "menu_unit_head_staff"))
        bot.handle_callbacks(DummyCall(uh, "menu_unit_head_attendance"))
        print("  âœ… ظپط±ط¢غŒظ†ط¯ ظ…ط³ط¦ظˆظ„ ظˆط§ط­ط¯ ظˆ ط«ط¨طھ ع©ظ…ط¨ظˆط¯ ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ غ±غ°غ°ظھ طھط³طھ ط´ط¯.")

    def test_03_admin_shortage_approval_and_custom_shortages(self):
        """طھط³طھ ع©ط§ط±طھط§ط¨ظ„ ط§ط¯ظ…غŒظ† ط¬ظ‡طھ طھط§غŒغŒط¯طŒ ط±ط¯ ظˆ ظˆغŒط±ط§غŒط´ ع©ظ…ط¨ظˆط¯ظ‡ط§ ظˆ ط§غŒط¬ط§ط¯ ع©ظ…ط¨ظˆط¯ ط¬ط¯غŒط¯"""
        print("\nًں”¹ طھط³طھ غ³: ط¨ط±ط±ط³غŒطŒ طھط§غŒغŒط¯ ظˆ ط±ط¯ ع©ظ…ط¨ظˆط¯ ظ†غŒط±ظˆ طھظˆط³ط· ط§ط¯ظ…غŒظ†...")
        admin = self.admin_id
        
        # ع©ط§ط±طھط§ط¨ظ„ ظ…ط´ط§ظ‡ط¯ظ‡ ع©ظ…ط¨ظˆط¯ظ‡ط§
        bot.handle_callbacks(DummyCall(admin, "menu_view_shortages"))
        
        shortages = shortage_manager.get_all_unresolved_shortages(self.pid)
        self.assertTrue(len(shortages) > 0, "At least one shortage should exist")
        sh_id = shortages[-1]['id']
        
        # ظˆغŒط±ط§غŒط´ طھط¹ط¯ط§ط¯ ع©ظ…ط¨ظˆط¯ طھظˆط³ط· ط§ط¯ظ…غŒظ†
        bot.handle_callbacks(DummyCall(admin, f"edit_uh_count_{sh_id}_{self.uh_tadarakat}"))
        bot.process_admin_edit_uh_count(DummyMessage(admin, "1"), sh_id, self.uh_tadarakat)
        
        # طھط§غŒغŒط¯ ع©ظ…ط¨ظˆط¯
        bot.handle_callbacks(DummyCall(admin, f"appr_uh_shrt_{sh_id}_{self.uh_tadarakat}"))
        
        # ط«ط¨طھ ع©ظ…ط¨ظˆط¯ ظ…ط³طھظ‚غŒظ… طھظˆط³ط· ط§ط¯ظ…غŒظ† ط§ط±ط´ط¯
        sa = self.super_admin_id
        bot.handle_callbacks(DummyCall(sa, "menu_shortage"))
        bot.handle_callbacks(DummyCall(sa, "shrt_unit_طھط¯ط§ط±ع©ط§طھ"))
        bot.process_shortage_section(DummyMessage(sa, "ط¯ظ¾ظˆ"))
        bot.process_shortage_count(DummyMessage(sa, "3"))
        bot.process_shortage_desc(DummyMessage(sa, "ع©ظ…ط¨ظˆط¯ ظ¾ط´طھغŒط¨ط§ظ†غŒ ط®ط§ظ†ظ…â€Œظ‡ط§"))
        bot.handle_callbacks(DummyCall(sa, "shrt_grp_ط®ط§ظ†ظ…"))
        
        # ظ¾غŒط´ظ†ظ‡ط§ط¯ ظ†غŒط±ظˆ ط¨ط±ط§غŒ ع©ظ…ط¨ظˆط¯
        bot.handle_callbacks(DummyCall(sa, f"suggestshrt_{sh_id}"))
        bot.process_suggest_shrt_name(DummyMessage(sa, "ظ…ط­ظ…ط¯ط±ط¶ط§ ع©ط§ط¸ظ…غŒ"))
        bot.process_suggest_shrt_phone(DummyMessage(sa, "09123334444"))
        
        # طھط§غŒغŒط¯ ظˆ ط±ط¯ ظ¾غŒط´ظ†ظ‡ط§ط¯
        bot.handle_callbacks(DummyCall(sa, f"approveshrt_{sh_id}"))
        bot.handle_callbacks(DummyCall(sa, f"rejectshrt_{sh_id}"))
        print("  âœ… ظپط±ط¢غŒظ†ط¯ظ‡ط§غŒ طھط§غŒغŒط¯ ظˆ ظ…ط¯غŒط±غŒطھ ع©ظ…ط¨ظˆط¯ظ‡ط§ ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ طھط³طھ ط´ط¯.")

    def test_04_attendance_and_cards_workflows(self):
        """طھط³طھ ط«ط¨طھ ط­ط¶ظˆط±طŒ ط؛غŒط§ط¨طŒ ظ¾غŒع¯غŒط±غŒ طھط£ط®غŒط± ظˆ ع©ط§ط±طھâ€Œظ‡ط§"""
        print("\nًں”¹ طھط³طھ غ´: طھط³طھ ع©ط§ظ…ظ„ ط­ط¶ظˆط± ظˆ ط؛غŒط§ط¨ ظˆ ع©ط§ط±طھâ€Œظ‡ط§...")
        op = self.operator_id
        
        # ظ…ظ†ظˆغŒ ظˆط§ط­ط¯ظ‡ط§
        bot.handle_callbacks(DummyCall(op, "menu_units"))
        bot.handle_callbacks(DummyCall(op, "up_0:طھط¯ط§ط±ع©ط§طھ"))
        
        # ط¨ط§ط² ع©ط±ط¯ظ† ظ¾ط±ظˆظپط§غŒظ„ ط¹ظ„غŒ ط±ط¶ط§غŒغŒ
        bot.handle_callbacks(DummyCall(op, f"user_{self.st1}"))
        
        # طھط؛غŒغŒط± ظˆط¶ط¹غŒطھâ€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ظ…ط®طھظ„ظپ ط­ط¶ظˆط±
        for st_val in ["ط­ط§ط¶ط±", "ط؛ط§غŒط¨", "ط¨ط¯ظˆظ† ط´غŒظپطھ", "clear"]:
            bot.handle_callbacks(DummyCall(op, f"act_status_{self.st1}_{st_val}"))
            
        # طھط؛غŒغŒط± ظˆط¶ط¹غŒطھâ€Œظ‡ط§غŒ ع©ط§ط±طھ
        for card_val in ["طھط­ظˆغŒظ„ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡ ط´ط¯", "ظ¾ط³ ع¯ط±ظپطھظ‡ ط´ط¯", "ط¨ط¯ظˆظ† ع©ط§ط±طھ", "clear"]:
            bot.handle_callbacks(DummyCall(op, f"act_card_{self.st1}_{card_val}"))
            
        # ظ¾غŒع¯غŒط±غŒ طھط£ط®غŒط±
        bot.handle_callbacks(DummyCall(op, f"tracklate_{self.st1}"))
        bot.process_track_late(DummyMessage(op, "طھظ…ط§ط³ ع¯ط±ظپطھظ‡ ط´ط¯طŒ ظ†غŒظ… ط³ط§ط¹طھ ط¯غŒع¯ط± ظ…غŒâ€Œط±ط³ط¯"))
        
        # ظˆغŒط±ط§غŒط´ طھظˆط¶غŒط­ط§طھ
        bot.handle_callbacks(DummyCall(op, f"editdesc_{self.st1}"))
        bot.process_edit_desc(DummyMessage(op, "ع©ط§ط±طھ ظ…ظˆظ‚طھ طھط­ظˆغŒظ„ ع¯ط±ط¯غŒط¯"))
        
        # طµظپط­ظ‡ ظ„غŒط³طھ ظ…طھط£ط®ط±غŒظ†
        bot.handle_callbacks(DummyCall(op, "menu_late_page_0"))
        bot.handle_callbacks(DummyCall(op, "menu_late_page_1"))
        print("  âœ… ط¹ظ…ظ„غŒط§طھ ط­ط¶ظˆط± ظˆ ع©ط§ط±طھ ظˆ طھظˆط¶غŒط­ط§طھ ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط«ط¨طھ ط´ط¯ظ†ط¯.")

    def test_05_sessions_and_group_shift(self):
        """طھط³طھ طھط؛غŒغŒط± ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ ظپط¹ط§ظ„طŒ ط§غŒط¬ط§ط¯ ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ ط¬ط¯غŒط¯طŒ ظ„ط؛ظˆ ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡ ظˆ ط´غŒظپطھ ع¯ط±ظˆظ‡غŒ"""
        print("\nًں”¹ طھط³طھ غµ: ظ…ط¯غŒط±غŒطھ ط¬ظ„ط³ط§طھ ظˆ ط´غŒظپطھ ط³ط§ط¹طھغŒ ع¯ط±ظˆظ‡غŒ...")
        sa = self.super_admin_id
        
        bot.handle_callbacks(DummyCall(sa, "menu_manage_sessions"))
        bot.handle_callbacks(DummyCall(sa, "menu_change_session"))

=== utils.py ===
import re
from datetime import datetime
from config import LATE_THRESHOLD_SECONDS


def normalize_persian(text):
    if text is None:
        return ""
    text = str(text)
    replacements = {
        'ظٹ': 'غŒ',
        'ظƒ': 'ع©',
        'ط©': 'ظ‡',
        'ط¤': 'ظˆ',
        'ط¥': 'ط§',
        'ط£': 'ط§',
        '\u200c': '',
        'ظ ': '0', 'ظ،': '1', 'ظ¢': '2', 'ظ£': '3', 'ظ¤': '4',
        'ظ¥': '5', 'ظ¦': '6', 'ظ§': '7', 'ظ¨': '8', 'ظ©': '9',
        'غ°': '0', 'غ±': '1', 'غ²': '2', 'غ³': '3', 'غ´': '4',
        'غµ': '5', 'غ¶': '6', 'غ·': '7', 'غ¸': '8', 'غ¹': '9',
    }
    for old_char, new_char in replacements.items():
        text = text.replace(old_char, new_char)
    return text.strip()


def safe_markdown(text):
    if text is None:
        return ""
    s = str(text)
    for ch in ['_', '*', '`', '[']:
        s = s.replace(ch, f'\\{ch}')
    return s


def get_user_emojis(status, card_status):
    st = str(status or "").strip()
    cd = str(card_status or "").strip()

    if "ط­ط§ط¶ط±" in st:
        att_icon = "ًںں¢"
    elif "ط؛ط§غŒط¨" in st:
        att_icon = "â‌Œ"
    elif "طھط§ط®غŒط±" in st:
        att_icon = "âڑ ï¸ڈ"
    elif "ط¨ط¯ظˆظ† ط´غŒظپطھ" in st:
        att_icon = "âڑھï¸ڈ"
    else:
        att_icon = "â‌”"

    if "طھط­ظˆغŒظ„ ط¯ط§ط¯ظ‡ ط´ط¯" in cd:
        card_icon = "ًں’³"
    elif "ظ¾ط³ ع¯ط±ظپطھظ‡ ط´ط¯" in cd or "طھط­ظˆغŒظ„ ع¯ط±ظپطھظ‡ ط´ط¯" in cd:
        card_icon = "ًں”„"
    elif "ط¨ط¯ظˆظ† ع©ط§ط±طھ" in cd:
        card_icon = "âڑھï¸ڈ"
    else:
        card_icon = "â–«ï¸ڈ"

    return f"{att_icon}{card_icon}"


def format_delay_minutes(shift_time_str, now=None):
    if not shift_time_str or ":" not in str(shift_time_str):
        return 0
    if now is None:
        now = datetime.now()
    clean_time = normalize_persian(str(shift_time_str))
    match = re.search(r'(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})', clean_time)
    if not match:
        return 0
    try:
        sh_hour = int(match.group(1))
        sh_min = int(match.group(2))
        shift_dt = now.replace(hour=sh_hour, minute=sh_min, second=0, microsecond=0)
        diff_seconds = (now - shift_dt).total_seconds()
        if diff_seconds <= 0:
            return 0
        return int(diff_seconds // 60)
    except Exception:
        return 0


def calculate_status_with_delay(shift_time_str, now=None, threshold_seconds=None):
    if not shift_time_str or ":" not in str(shift_time_str):
        return "ط­ط§ط¶ط±"
    if now is None:
        now = datetime.now()
    if threshold_seconds is None:
        threshold_seconds = LATE_THRESHOLD_SECONDS

    clean_time = normalize_persian(str(shift_time_str))
    match = re.search(r'(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})', clean_time)
    if not match:
        return "ط­ط§ط¶ط±"

    try:
        sh_hour = int(match.group(1))
        sh_min = int(match.group(2))
        shift_dt = now.replace(hour=sh_hour, minute=sh_min, second=0, microsecond=0)
        diff_seconds = (now - shift_dt).total_seconds()
        if diff_seconds > threshold_seconds:
            delay_mins = int(diff_seconds // 60)
            return f"ط­ط§ط¶ط± (ط¨ط§ {delay_mins} ط¯ظ‚غŒظ‚ظ‡ طھط§ط®غŒط±)"
        return "ط­ط§ط¶ط±"
    except Exception:
        return "ط­ط§ط¶ط±"


def get_persian_weekday(dt=None):
    if dt is None:
        dt = datetime.now()
    weekdays = {
        5: "ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡",
        6: "غŒع©ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡",
        0: "ط¯ظˆط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡",
        1: "ط³ظ‡â€Œط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡",
        2: "ع†ظ‡ط§ط±ط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡",
        3: "ظ¾ظ†ط¬â€Œط´ظ†ط¨ظ‡",
        4: "ط¬ظ…ط¹ظ‡"
    }
    return weekdays.get(dt.weekday(), "")
=== worker_manager.py ===
import os
import sys
import time
import threading
import logging
from datetime import datetime
from config import (
    DB_BACKUP_INTERVAL, LATE_REMINDER_INTERVAL, SHORTAGE_CHECK_INTERVAL,
    DAILY_REPORT_HOUR, DAILY_REPORT_MINUTE
)
from db_manager import db_instance
from project_manager import project_manager
from attendance_manager import attendance_manager
from shortage_manager import shortage_manager
from permission_manager import permission_manager
from report_manager import report_manager
from excel_manager import excel_manager


class WorkerManager:
    def __init__(self, db=db_instance):
        self.db = db
        self.bot = None
        self._running = False
        self._threads = []
        self._last_daily_report_date = None

    def set_bot(self, bot_instance):
        self.bot = bot_instance

    def get_today_sessions(self, project_id):
        proj = project_manager.get_project(project_id)
        if not proj or proj.get('status') != 'ACTIVE':
            return []

        today_str = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        conn = self.db.get_connection()
        try:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("""
            SELECT s.*
            FROM sessions s
            LEFT JOIN schedules sc ON s.schedule_id = sc.id
            WHERE s.project_id = %s
              AND s.session_date = %s
              AND s.status != 'CANCELLED'
              AND (s.schedule_id IS NULL OR sc.is_active = TRUE)
            ORDER BY s.id ASC
            """, (project_id, today_str))
            rows = cursor.fetchall()
            return [dict(r) for r in rows]
        except Exception as e:
            logging.error(f"Error in get_today_sessions for project {project_id}: {e}")
            return []
        finally:
            conn.close()

    def start_workers(self):
        if self._running:
            return
        self._running = True

        worker_targets = [
            ("ShortageWorker", self._shortage_loop),
            ("LateReminderWorker", self._late_reminder_loop),
            ("BackupWorker", self._backup_loop),
            ("DailyReportWorker", self._daily_report_loop),
        ]

        for name, target in worker_targets:
            t = threading.Thread(target=target, name=name, daemon=True)
            t.start()
            self._threads.append(t)

    def stop_workers(self):
        self._running = False

    def _shortage_loop(self):
        while self._running:
            try:
                if self.bot:
                    unnotified = shortage_manager.get_unnotified_shortages()
                    for sh in unnotified:
                        pid = sh['project_id']
                        pname = sh.get('project_name', '')
                        grp = sh.get('target_group') or 'ط¹ظ…ظˆظ…غŒ'
                        msg = (
                            f"ًں”” **ط§ط¹ظ„ط§ظ… ع©ظ…ط¨ظˆط¯ ظ†غŒط±ظˆ - {pname}**\n\n"
                            f"ًںڈ¢ ظˆط§ط­ط¯: **{sh['unit']}**\n"
                            f"ًں—‚ ط¨ط®ط´: **{sh['section']}**\n"
                            f"ًں‘¥ ط¬ظ†ط³غŒطھ: **{grp}**\n"
                            f"ًں“‌ طھظˆط¶غŒط­ط§طھ: {sh.get('description') or 'ظ†ط¯ط§ط±ط¯'}\n\n"
                            f"ظ„ط·ظپط§ظ‹ ط¬ظ‡طھ ظ…ط¹ط±ظپغŒ ظ†غŒط±ظˆغŒ ط¬ط§غŒع¯ط²غŒظ† ط§ط² ظ…ظ†ظˆغŒ ط±ط¨ط§طھ ط§ظ‚ط¯ط§ظ… ظپط±ظ…ط§غŒغŒط¯."
                        )
                        members = permission_manager.get_project_members(pid)
                        for m in members:
                            gender_match = (grp == 'ط¹ظ…ظˆظ…غŒ') or (m.get('project_gender') == grp)
                            if m.get('is_active') and m.get('role') != 'unit_head' and (
                                m.get('role') in ('admin', 'super_admin') or gender_match
                            ):
                                if not permission_manager.is_operator_absent_today(pid, m['user_id']):
                                    try:
                                        self.bot.send_message(m['user_id'], msg, parse_mode="Markdown")
                                    except Exception:
                                        pass
                        shortage_manager.mark_shortage_notified(sh['id'])
            except Exception as e:
                logging.error(f"ShortageWorker error: {e}")
            time.sleep(max(5, SHORTAGE_CHECK_INTERVAL))

    def _late_reminder_loop(self):
        while self._running:
            time.sleep(max(60, LATE_REMINDER_INTERVAL))
            try:
                if not self.bot:
                    continue
                active_projects = project_manager.list_projects(status='ACTIVE')
                for proj in active_projects:
                    pid = proj['id']
                    today_sessions = self.get_today_sessions(pid)
                    for sess in today_sessions:
                        sid = sess['id']
                        latecomers = attendance_manager.get_latecomers(pid, sid)
                        untracked = [u for u in latecomers if not str(u.get('late_tracking', '')).strip()]
                        if not untracked:
                            continue

                        members = permission_manager.get_project_members(pid)
                        for m in members:
                            if not m.get('is_active') or m.get('role') == 'unit_head':
                                continue
                            uid = m['user_id']
                            if permission_manager.is_operator_absent_today(pid, uid):
                                continue

                            user_late_list = permission_manager.apply_gender_filter(untracked, uid, pid)
                            if user_late_list:
                                msg = (
                                    f"ًںڑ¨ **غŒط§ط¯ط¢ظˆط±غŒ ظ¾غŒع¯غŒط±غŒ ظ…طھط£ط®ط±غŒظ† - {proj['name']}**\n"
                                    f"ًں“… ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡: **{sess['name']}**\n\n"
                                    f"طھط¹ط¯ط§ط¯ **{len(user_late_list)} ظ†ظپط±** ط§ط² ظ†غŒط±ظˆظ‡ط§غŒ ط´غŒظپطھ ظ‡ظ†ظˆط² ظˆط¶ط¹غŒطھ ط­ط¶ظˆط±ط´ط§ظ† ط«ط¨طھ ظ†ط´ط¯ظ‡ ظˆ ظ¾غŒع¯غŒط±غŒ ظ‡ظ… ط¨ط±ط§غŒط´ط§ظ† ط¯ط±ط¬ ظ†ع¯ط±ط¯غŒط¯ظ‡ ط§ط³طھ.\n"
                                    f"ظ„ط·ظپط§ظ‹ ط§ط² ط¨ط®ط´ آ«ظ„غŒط³طھ ظ…طھط£ط®ط±غŒظ†آ» ظ¾غŒع¯غŒط±غŒ ظپط±ظ…ط§غŒغŒط¯."
                                )
                                try:
                                    self.bot.send_message(uid, msg, parse_mode="Markdown")
                                except Exception:
                                    pass
            except Exception as e:
                logging.error(f"LateReminderWorker error: {e}")

    def _backup_loop(self):
        while self._running:
            time.sleep(max(300, DB_BACKUP_INTERVAL))
            try:
                db_instance.backup_database(label="periodic")
                active_projects = project_manager.list_projects(status='ACTIVE')
                for proj in active_projects:
                    try:
                        excel_manager.backup_project_excel(proj['id'], label="periodic")
                    except Exception as ex_err:
                        logging.warning(f"Periodic excel backup warning for project {proj['id']}: {ex_err}")
            except Exception as e:
                logging.error(f"BackupWorker error: {e}")

    def _daily_report_loop(self):
        while self._running:
            try:
                now = datetime.now()
                today_str = now.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
                if (now.hour == DAILY_REPORT_HOUR and now.minute >= DAILY_REPORT_MINUTE and
                        self._last_daily_report_date != today_str):
                    self._last_daily_report_date = today_str
                    if self.bot:
                        active_projects = project_manager.list_projects(status='ACTIVE')
                        for proj in active_projects:
                            pid = proj['id']
                            today_sessions = self.get_today_sessions(pid)
                            for sess in today_sessions:
                                sid = sess['id']
                                attendance_manager.mark_empty_as_absent(pid, sid)
                                stats = report_manager.get_dashboard_stats(pid, sid)
                                summary = (
                                    f"ًںŒ™ **ع¯ط²ط§ط±ط´ ط¬ظ…ط¹â€Œط¨ظ†ط¯غŒ ط´ط¨ط§ظ†ظ‡ - {proj['name']}**\n"
                                    f"ًں“… ط¬ظ„ط³ظ‡: **{sess['name']}**\n\n"
                                    f"ًں‘¥ ع©ظ„ ظ…ظˆط±ط¯ ط§ظ†طھط¸ط§ط±: {stats['total_expected']}\n"
                                    f"âœ… ع©ظ„ ط­ط§ط¶ط±غŒظ†: {stats['total_present']}\n"
                                    f"ًں“ٹ ط¯ط±طµط¯ ظ…ط´ط§ط±ع©طھ: **{stats['overall_percentage']}%**"
                                )
                                admins = permission_manager.get_project_admins(pid, include_global=True)
                                for adm in admins:
                                    try:
                                        self.bot.send_message(adm['user_id'], summary, parse_mode="Markdown")
                                    except Exception:
                                        pass
            except Exception as e:
                logging.error(f"DailyReportWorker error: {e}")
            time.sleep(30)


worker_manager = WorkerManager()
